However, it introduces a critical challenge: model-induced label noise. Unlike stochastic noise in classical robust learning, this noise stems from annotator inductive biases, manifesting as systematic errors tightly coupled with local feature manifolds. Existing methods relying on global transition matrices underfit these structural patterns, while learning instance-specific matrices remains mathematically intractable. We propose Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND), a theoretically grounded framework addressing this dilemma. We demonstrate that the high-dimensional noise manifold can be decoupled into tractable, subspace-dependent components via Latent Manifold Disentanglement. Specifically, our Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE) dynamically projects samples into latent structural clusters with consistent error modes, facilitating noise identifiability without ground-truth anchor points. To rigorously evaluate robustness, we adopt a hierarchical protocol: moving from controlled noise on CIFAR-100 to a structural stress test on large-scale real-world 3D datasets (S3DIS, ScanNet), where error patterns explicitly couple with geometric manifolds. Empirically, MIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on these complex benchmarks and effectively corrects zero-shot hallucinations from Vision-Language Models (e.g., OpenSeg), highlighting its potential as a robust distillation framework for Foundation Models.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-level Self-supervised Pretraining on Compositional Hierarchical Graph for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16088)
*Xiayu Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Hou-biao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolCHG是一个基于层级组合图的多层次自监督预训练框架，通过将分子结构组织为三个语义层次的四种节点类型，并引入与原子图并行的键图，实现键级信息的独立节点表征。在九个MoleculeNet基准测试中，MolCHG在七个数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子图自监督预训练方法大多在单一结构粒度上操作，将键信息仅视为辅助边属性而非独立语义层，限制了模型捕获丰富化学信息的能力。

Method: 提出MolCHG框架，构建包含四个节点类型（原子、键、片段、分子）的层级组合图；引入与原子图并行的键图以实现键级信息独立演化；设计三个层次特定的预训练任务：原子-键跨视角对比、片段级官能团预测、图级结构预测。

Result: 在九个MoleculeNet基准测试中，MolCHG在七个数据集上取得最佳性能；消融实验证实多级监督信号互补且各组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 将键信息提升为独立的节点表征层，并联合多层次的预训练监督信号，能有效提升分子性质预测性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining on molecular graphs has emerged as a promising approach for molecular property prediction, yet most existing methods operate at a single structural granularity and treat bond information as auxiliary edge attributes rather than as an independent semantic layer. In this work, we propose MolCHG, a multi-level self-supervised pretraining framework built upon a novel Compositional Hierarchical Graph that organizes molecular structure into four types of nodes across three semantic levels. By introducing a bond graph that operates in parallel with the atom graph, our architecture elevates bond-level information to independently evolving node representations, enabling fragment nodes to aggregate atom-level and bond-level semantics on an equal footing. We design three level-specific pretraining objectives: an atom-bond cross-view contrastive task that aligns the atom-view and bond-view representations within each fragment, a fragment-level functional group prediction task to inject domain-relevant chemical knowledge, and graph-level structure prediction tasks to encode global molecular topology. Experiments on nine MoleculeNet benchmarks demonstrate that MolCHG achieves the best performance on seven datasets across both classification and regression tasks, remaining competitive with the strongest baselines on the rest. Ablation studies further confirm that the multi-level supervision signals are complementary and that each component contributes to the overall performance.

</details>


### [111] [Centralized vs Decentralized Federated Learning: A trade-off performance analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16089)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Guilain Leduc,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fedstellar模拟器、MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，对集中式、去中心化和半去中心化三种联邦学习架构进行了实验比较分析，填补了该领域缺乏系统对比研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对集中式、去中心化和半去中心化三种联邦学习架构的系统性实验比较，无法全面了解各自优势、局限性及性能指标间的权衡关系。

Method: 使用Fedstellar模拟器，基于MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，对CFL、DFL、SDFL三种架构进行实验对比分析。

Result: 通过实验比较了三种FL架构在不同性能指标上的表现差异和权衡关系。

Conclusion: 不同联邦学习架构各有优劣，架构选择需根据具体应用需求权衡各项性能指标。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while preserving data privacy especially with the huge increase amount of data due to the adoption of technologies which contributes to the growing number of IoT devices. Storing this amount of data centrally is challenging due to issues like limited communication, privacy, and regulations. FL can be Centralized (CFL), Decentralized (DFL), and Semi-decentralized (SDFL). Choosing the right FL architecture depends on the application's needs. However, very few research studies have experimentally compared these three types of architectures to not only understand the respective strengths and limitations, but also trade-offs between different performance indicators. This paper overcome this lack of analysis, conducting experimental analyses using the Fedstellar simulator, MNIST dataset, and MLP classifier.

</details>


### [112] [Federated Imputation under Heterogeneous Feature Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16099)
*Imane Hocine,Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Gregoire Danoy,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedHF-Impute联邦学习框架，通过共享全局特征图的消息传递机制，解决表格数据中客户端特征模式部分重叠时的联合插补问题，在SECOM和AirQuality数据集上分别提升26.9%和8.4%的插补精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法（如FedAvg）假设客户端特征模式对齐，但在表格数据场景中客户端通常仅观察到部分重叠的特征子集，导致参数平均方法在弱重叠或不相交特征组间信息传递有限，无法有效进行联邦插补。

Method: 将结构性特征不可用性与传统缺失值区分开来，构建共享的全局特征图，通过消息传递机制在统计相关的特征间传播信息，实现跨客户端的间接知识迁移，同时保持标准联邦通信协议。

Result: 在SECOM数据集上提升26.9%，AirQuality数据集上提升8.4%，在PhysioNET上与最佳基线仅差0.3%。

Conclusion: FedHF-Impute通过全局特征图的消息传递有效解决了异构特征空间下的联邦插补问题，在部分特征重叠场景中显著优于现有FL基线方法。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training across decentralized clients, but most methods assume aligned feature schemas, an assumption that rarely holds in tabular settings where clients observe only partially overlapping feature subsets. In these heterogeneous feature spaces, parameter-averaging methods (e.g., FedAvg) transfer little information across weakly overlapping or disjoint feature groups, limiting their effectiveness for federated imputation. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedHF-Impute}, a federated imputation framework that separates structural feature unavailability from conventional missingness and uses a shared global feature graph to propagate information across statistically related features through message passing. This enables indirect cross-client knowledge transfer, even when features are never jointly observed locally, while preserving standard federated communication. Under simulated partial schema overlap on the SECOM and AirQuality datasets, FedHF-Impute improves imputation accuracy (RMSE) over FL baselines by 26.9\%, and 8.4\% respectively, while achieving comparable performance on PhysioNET, with only a 0.3\% difference relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [113] [Attention Dispersion in Dynamic Graph Transformers: Diagnosis and a Transferable Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16112)
*Jinhao Zhang,Kangfei Zhao,Qiuhao Zeng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现动态图Transformer在时间分布偏移下会出现注意力分散问题，导致无法聚焦关键节点；提出用微分注意力（differential attention）替代标准注意力，通过抑制共模注意力、放大判别性信号来提升性能。基于该机制构建的DiffDyG在9个基准上达到SOTA，尤其在高偏移数据集上增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的连续时间动态图学习方法在时间分布偏移场景下性能受限，但根本原因尚不明确。本文旨在诊断该场景下模型的失效模式，并设计有效的改进方案。

Method: 1) 通过控制消融实验（对比结构/时间区分性历史邻居与随机邻居）识别关键节点；2) 诊断发现标准注意力在时间偏移下产生过度分散的注意力分布（注意力分散）；3) 提出用微分注意力替代标准注意力，抑制共模注意力、增强判别性token级信号；4) 基于此构建DiffDyG参考实现。

Result: 微分注意力可一致提升三种代表性CTDG Transformer基线在高偏移数据集上的性能；DiffDyG在9个基准和三种负采样协议下达到SOTA，注意力测量显示其降低了注意力熵并增加了关键节点上的注意力质量。

Conclusion: 注意力分散是动态图Transformer在时间分布偏移下的核心失效模式，微分注意力是一种简单且可迁移的修复方案，能有效提升模型在时间偏移场景下的鲁棒性和预测性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have become the dominant paradigm for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) learning, yet their performance remains limited on temporally shifted datasets. In this work, we identify attention dispersion as a shared failure mode of dynamic graph Transformers under temporal distribution shift. Through controlled ablation contrasting structurally and temporally distinguished historical neighbors against random ones, we show that prediction depends on a class of critical nodes that carry consistently more predictive signal than arbitrary neighbors. However, existing Transformers fail to focus on these nodes even when they are present in the input, as temporal shift weakens attention contrast and produces overly dispersed attention distributions. This diagnosis suggests a simple and transferable fix: replace standard attention with differential attention, which suppresses common-mode attention and amplifies distinctive token-level signals. When added to three representative CTDG Transformer baselines, differential attention consistently improves performance, with gains concentrated on high-shift datasets. Attention-level measurements further confirm the mechanism, showing reduced attention entropy and increased attention mass on critical nodes. Building on these findings, we introduce DiffDyG, a reference implementation combining differential attention with standard input encodings. Across 9 benchmarks and three negative sampling protocols, DiffDyG achieves SOTA performance, with especially large gains on the most shifted datasets.

</details>


### [114] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于流的生成模型在低推理预算下的采样问题，提出了一种基于条件-边际熵率的目标函数来指导离散化网格设计，无训练地生成符合桥过程几何特性的调度策略。在多个任务（2D桥模型、CIFAR-10、AlphaFlow蛋白质生成）上，该方法在少量函数评估（NFE）设置下显著优于线性、余弦等启发式网格，验证了熵率调度作为低预算分配信号的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定小推理预算下，基于流的生成模型的采样质量高度依赖于函数评估的分配位置，而现有方法通常使用启发式网格或继承自扩散模型的调度，缺乏对桥过程自身几何特性的考虑。

Method: 推导了条件-边际熵率目标函数，将端点条件桥几何与边际流演化解耦；对于高斯布朗桥，熵率有闭式解且呈U形，据此设计边界密集的非均匀网格；无需额外训练，直接应用于推理阶段的调度生成。

Result: 在2D桥模型上，10步ODE-Heun MMD较线性提升18.1%，SDE-Heun提升22.7%；在EDM/CIFAR-10上，5步FID达到186.3（线性200.5，余弦238.0）；在AlphaFlow蛋白质生成上，低NFE场景下在CAMEO22和ATLAS基准上均表现出优势。

Conclusion: 熵率调度作为一种实用的低预算分配信号，能够有效提升高维桥和流采样器在小推理预算下的生成质量。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [115] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLQR+SAM结合了SAM和LLQR预条件器，通过双时间尺度机制（慢速学习几何 + 快速锐度校正）在平坦盆地中保持稳定，同时放大局部尖锐“坑洞”方向的逃逸信号，在视觉和序列建模任务上优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAM对所有参数方向均匀扰动，忽略了损失曲面的几何结构，导致对局部几何信息的利用不足。

Method: 将LLQR框架学习到的预条件器作为慢速指数移动平均，捕捉平滑的低分辨率几何信息；SAM在此基础上以更快时间尺度进行扰动探测曲率，形成双时间尺度结构。

Result: 在标准视觉和序列建模基准上，LLQR+SAM一致优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。

Conclusion: 慢速学习的损失几何与快速锐度校正确实具有互补性，预条件器能放大平坦几何下局部尖锐方向的逃逸信号，同时保持宽平坦盆地的稳定性。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [116] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵自编码器(EAE)，通过隐变量的隐式先验分布和自由能最小化集成编码器来缓解变分自编码器(VAE)的后验坍塌问题，在多个数据集上证明其能学习多模态、非高斯隐分布并保留数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE普遍存在后验坍塌问题，即隐变量被有效忽略。这是由于显式先验的施加将优化过程推向对应于无信息隐表示的损失景观区域。

Method: 提出熵自编码器(EAE)框架：仅以重构损失为显式目标，通过自由能最小化的编码器集成隐式生成隐变量的先验分布。编码器集成将学习偏向于近最优解的高体积区域，解码器更新则将搜索轨迹引向信息性隐表示。

Result: EAE能缓解后验坍塌，学习非高斯多模态隐分布，产生多样且数据一致的生成结果。在反应扩散过程中捕捉已知的低维动力学叠加；在MNIST中识别隐式类别区分；在CelebA上展示从"全人类"脸到个体依赖特征的分层面部结构理解。

Conclusion: EAE通过隐式先验和集成学习有效解决了VAE的后验坍塌问题，能够学习更丰富、更具结构保留能力的隐表示。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [117] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAPR算法，融合贝叶斯在线变化检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成强化学习，解决分段平稳环境下全局保守策略与局部自适应策略的两难困境。通过冻结信念分布的凸组合构造γ-压缩算子，并形式化验证了压缩因子的严格边界（γ+λΔ<1），所有结果均在Lean 4中机器验证（1,145行代码，22条定理）。BOCD驱动的自适应保守机制在检测到变化点后最大化保守性，随置信度增长平滑放松，检测延迟O(log(1/δ))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际控制系统常处于分段平稳状态（长时间稳定后突发变化），标准鲁棒强化学习方法面临根本性困境：全局保守策略在稳定期性能浪费，而局部自适应策略在未检测到变化时可能导致灾难性失败。现有方法无法同时兼顾稳定期性能与变化期鲁棒性。

Method: 提出BAPR算法：(1) 将贝叶斯在线变化检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成强化学习融合；(2) 构造BAPR算子——由冻结信念分布加权的模式条件Bellman算子的凸组合，证明为γ-压缩算子；(3) 通过Lean 4形式化验证压缩因子的严格边界：当信念依赖Q函数时，压缩因子为γ+λΔ，失败条件为γ+λΔ≥1；(4) 推导分量式形式化误差预算；(5) BOCD驱动自适应保守机制，检测延迟O(log(1/δ))；(6) 上下文条件模块通过RMDM损失训练，训练时使用模拟器提供的模式ID，部署时无需模式标签。

Result: BAPR算子是γ-压缩算子；严格边界成立：当信念依赖Q函数时，压缩因子为γ+λΔ，且当γ+λΔ≥1时压缩失败；所有结果在Lean 4中完成机器验证（1,145行代码，3个文件，22条定理，无sorry）；检测延迟为O(log(1/δ))。

Conclusion: BAPR通过融合贝叶斯变化检测与鲁棒强化学习，在分段平稳环境下实现了自适应保守策略，兼顾稳定期性能与变化期鲁棒性。形式化验证确保了理论结果的可靠性。上下文条件模块在部署时无需模式标签，增强了实用性。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [118] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对儿科ECMO（体外膜肺氧合）治疗中决策建模的高复杂性和数据稀缺问题，将临床决策制定形式化为从轨迹中学习行为的模仿学习任务（行为未直接观测），采用TabPFN（基于transformer的表格数据方法）与XGBoost、MLP等传统基线进行对比，实验结果表明TabPFN方法持续优于传统基线，可作为儿科ECMO决策支持的强临床行为基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科重症监护是动态、高风险的过程，涉及持续监测和生命维持治疗的调整，建模这些干预措施对有效决策支持至关重要。儿科ECMO面临高复杂性和数据稀缺的挑战，且行为未被直接观测。

Method: 将临床决策制定形式化为从轨迹中学习行为的模仿学习（imitation learning），使用TabPFN（基于transformer的表格数据方法）以及XGBoost、MLP等传统基线，在真实世界儿科ECMO数据上学习行为模型。

Result: TabPFN方法持续优于XGBoost和MLP等传统基线方法。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为儿科ECMO决策支持的强临床行为基线。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [119] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义昂萨格原理的数学约束假设类，用于学习介观动力学，该框架统一了耗散和保守动力学，并在学习前就提供了全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一分解可识别性和离散能量耗散等先验理论保证。通过数据驱动识别假设类中的成员，实现对复杂系统（如多尺度系统）的准确、鲁棒和可解释动力学建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学建模通常从固定的实例级有效方程出发，再进行特定方程的分析和计算，这一过程在复杂应用（如多尺度系统）中极具挑战性。作者希望提出一种替代范式，通过学习数学约束假设类中的介观动力学来克服这一困难。

Method: 基于广义昂萨格原理（generalized Onsager principle），构建一个统一的数学框架，涵盖耗散和保守介观动力学。该假设类在学习阶段之前就具有统一的理论保证（全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一分解可识别性、离散能量耗散）。然后利用每个问题实例的数据来指导识别假设类中的具体成员。

Result: 在连续PDE模型数据（作为验证）和微观链模型数据（精确介观模型未知）上进行了实证验证，结果表明该方法既能有效学习动力学，又能提供对底层物理的可解释诊断信息。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅是一种有效的动力学学习器，还能提供关于底层物理的重要可解释诊断，为复杂系统建模提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [120] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了差分隐私下条件风险价值（CVaR）学习的有效样本量、隐私成本与误差率，给出了标量估计、有限类及凸Lipschitz学习的完备速率界，揭示了私有CVaR学习的核心困难子问题等价于对Θ(nτ)个尾部记录进行普通私有学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解差分隐私对CVaR学习有效样本量的影响，并给出各类设置下私有CVaR学习误差的精确收敛速率。

Method: 通过有效样本量分析，将私有CVaR过剩风险分解为普通尾部风险统计误差与隐私代价两部分；在标量估计、有限类及凸Lipschitz学习三种设置下推导上下界，并给出纯DP下的完备速率及近似DP小δ下的下界扩展。

Result: 标量估计速率为Θ(B min{1, (nτ)^{-1/2} + (εnτ)^{-1}})；大小为M的有限类速率为Θ(B min{1, √[log(2M)/(nτ)] + log(2M)/(εnτ)})；凸Lipschitz学习中CVaR隐私项必然以1/(εnτ)量级缩放，维度依赖继承自私有随机凸优化。

Conclusion: 私有CVaR学习的核心困难在于对Θ(nτ)个尾部记录进行普通私有学习，隐私相关样本量为nτ，等效私有尾部样本量为εnτ。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [121] [Layer Equivalence Is Not a Property of Layers Alone: How You Test Redundancy Changes What You Find](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16234)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了两种衡量Transformer层等价性的协议——替换（Replacement）和互换（Interchange）——在评估层剪枝安全性时存在显著差异。在多个架构和规模（Pythia 410M/1.4B、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B）上，这种协议差距会随训练增长，且可能导致剪枝安全性的评估结果相差数倍。建议在实际层移除前，对两个指标同时进行诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在询问Transformer层是否"等价"以用于压缩时，常将不同测试混为一谈。作者旨在厘清替换（Replacement）与互换（Interchange）两种协议的本质区别，并量化它们在实际剪枝评估中导致的偏差。

Method: 在Pythia（410M和1.4B）训练轨迹、Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上，分别测量替换KL散度和互换KL散度，并对比两种协议在层剪枝安全性评估上的差异。仅需无标签前向传播即可完成诊断。

Result: 1) 替换-互换差距在训练过程中从初始化到收敛逐渐增大；2) 在Qwen3-8B上，互换引导的剪枝比替换引导安全数倍；3) Llama-3.1-8B上两个协议的剪枝成本相当，尽管互换KL更低，表明指标差距与移除效果并非一一对应。

Conclusion: 替换和互换是两种不同的输出接地swap-KL探测协议，它们不必一致。在进行层移除或合并之前，应同时评估这两种swap-KL指标。

Abstract: When researchers ask whether two transformer layers are "equivalent" for compression, they often conflate distinct tests. Replacement asks whether one layer's map can substitute for another's in place; interchange asks whether two layers approximately commute when their positions are swapped. Both are output-grounded swap-KL probes, but they need not agree: on pretrained transformers the protocol gap can change which layers look safe to prune by several-fold under the same evaluator, especially when replacement distances are high.
  We measure both protocols across checkpoints and architectures. On a Pythia training trajectory (410M and 1.4B), the replacement-interchange gap grows from initialization to convergence. Under one matched WikiText-2 contract at 8B scale, Qwen3-8B enters a divergent regime: interchange-guided removal is several-fold safer than replacement-guided at the same layer budgets, while Llama-3.1-8B ties the two protocols for pruning cost even though interchange KL is lower, showing metric gaps need not map one-to-one to removal. Before layer removal or merging, score both swap-KLs on the target checkpoint; the diagnostic requires only unlabeled forward passes.

</details>


### [122] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动力学层级的水印方法，将水印直接嵌入到流匹配模型的连续动力学（速度场）中，通过密钥相关的扰动进行训练，在检测时通过黑盒查询恢复消息，实验证明该方法能保持生成质量且无密钥时解码准确率接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型水印方法通常嵌入到模型权重或输出中，存在鲁棒性或隐蔽性不足的问题，本文希望探索一种更底层、更隐蔽的水印嵌入方式。

Method: 将水印嵌入到流匹配模型的连续动力学（速度场）中，采用随机编码方式在连续信道上传输：训练时添加密钥相关的扰动，检测时通过黑盒查询恢复消息，且扰动设计保证不改变生成分布。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，不同架构下均验证了可靠的消息恢复、生成质量保持不变，且无密钥时解码准确率接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 动力学层级的水印方法是一种有效且安全的生成模型水印方案，能够在不影响生成质量的前提下实现可靠的消息嵌入与恢复，且对无密钥攻击具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Fairness-Aware Retrieval Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15790)
*Yingqi Zhao,Vasilis Efthymiou,Jyrki Nummenmaa,Kostas Stefanidis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出FARO框架，通过重排序中的可控偏差注入、位置感知的偏差传播模型以及平衡相关性与公平性的优化公式，来缓解RAG系统中检索偏差对生成结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过引入外部知识提升LLM可靠性，但检索过程会引入偏差并传播到生成输出，特别是在top-k设置中多个文档共同影响生成时，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 1) 通过重排序实现可控偏差注入；2) 构建位置感知的偏差传播模型；3) 设计平衡相关性与公平性的优化目标；4) 提出基于对偶超平面近似的二次公平性优化（FARO）算法，通过问题分解实现高效求解。

Result: 该方法在有效缓解生成偏差的同时保持了相关性。

Conclusion: 为RAG系统中的公平感知检索提供了一种原则性方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves reliability of large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but the retrieval process can introduce bias that propagates to generated outputs. This issue is particularly challenging in top-k settings, where multiple documents jointly influence generation. We propose a fairness-aware retrieval framework that models and controls this bias. Our approach combines controlled bias injection via reranking, a position-aware model of bias propagation, and an optimization formulation that balances relevance and fairness. We further introduce a scalable solution based on Quadratic Fairness via Dual Hyperplane Approximation (FARO), which enables efficient optimization through problem decomposition. Experimental results show that our method effectively mitigates generation bias while preserving relevance. This work provides a principled approach for fairness-aware retrieval in RAG systems.

</details>


### [124] [To GPU or Not to GPU: Vector Search in Relational Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15957)
*Vasilis Mageirakos,Joel André,Marko Kabić,Bowen Wu,Yannis Chronis,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨在数据库引擎中集成GPU进行向量搜索的可行性，通过扩展TPC-H基准、构建CPU/GPU混合执行引擎，实验发现关系型组件在GPU上加速效果优于向量搜索本身，且数据迁移场景下GPU并非最优，但通过优化索引结构可使GPU方案更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库引擎在CPU上实现向量搜索，而AI/ML/LLM领域的主流计算平台是GPU，因此需要系统性地探究在数据库引擎中集成GPU进行向量处理是否更优。

Method: 扩展TPC-H基准加入向量数据并提出代表性SQL+VS查询；开发模块化执行引擎支持跨CPU/GPU执行；设计实验比较不同部署（CPU/GPU、内存位置、索引类型、互连方式）的性能。

Result: 关系型组件在GPU上加速效果比向量搜索更显著；向量搜索涉及数据和索引迁移时GPU并非最佳选择，即使使用NVLink等快速互连；通过优化向量索引结构减小索引体积，可使GPU方案更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 在快速互连条件下，优化后的GPU方案可使关系型和向量搜索组件均快于CPU，这与现有引擎架构相反，为数据库设计提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Vector search (VS) is now available in most database engines. However, while vector search is a common feature in AI/ML/LLMs where the dominant computing platforms are GPUs, existing database engines operate on CPUs even when implementing vector search. This raises the question of whether integrating vector processing on GPUs as part of the engine would be a better design. In this paper, we explore this question in detail. First, we extend the TPC-H benchmark with vector data (from text and images) and propose a number of representative SQL+VS queries. Second, we develop a modular execution engine that can run SQL+VS queries across CPU and GPU. Third, we perform extensive experiments on a number of deployments: running the SQL+VS queries across CPU and/or GPU, with data residing in CPU or GPU memory, with existing indices and novel, optimized versions, as well as across different GPUs and interconnects (PCIe, NVLink). The results provide actionable and counter-intuitive insights on how to run such queries over CPUs and GPUs. For instance, the relational components benefit much more from running on the GPU than the vector search part. In addition, when the vector search involves moving data and indexes, using the GPU is not the best option, even with fast interconnects. Thus, we develop an alternative organization of vector index and embeddings that reduces the size of the index, making GPU-based vector search more competitive. With these improvements, the final result is that both the relational and vector search components are faster on the GPU, particularly on fast interconnects, in contrast with the architecture used in existing engines.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Foundation Models for Relational Databases with Language Models and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16085)
*Jingcheng Wu,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Lucas Etteldorf,Max Finkenbeiner,Fabian Leeske,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合架构，结合微调后的BART编码器（捕获行内语义）和基于GraphSAGE的GNN（在关系实体图上注入关系上下文），用于关系数据库的深度学习。在RelBench的driver-dnf任务上达到67.40 ROC-AUC，性能优于LightGBM（68.86），与关系深度学习的差距缩小至5.22分，但与最先进的KumoRFM（82.63）仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库存储大量结构化信息，但现有深度学习方法通常通过手动特征工程将数据库扁平化为单表，丢失了关系上下文。而关系深度学习虽能建模关系实体图，但仍局限于特定任务和数据库。本文旨在结合两种范式的优势，探索轻量级混合LM-GNN架构作为关系数据库基础模型的可行性。

Method: 提出混合架构：1) 使用微调后的BART编码器捕获行内语义；2) 基于GraphSAGE的GNN在关系实体图上注入关系上下文；3) 在RelBench基准上评估。

Result: 在driver-dnf任务上达到67.40 ROC-AUC，优于监督基线LightGBM（68.86），与RDL（72.62）差距缩小至5.22分，但与KumoRFM（82.63）存在16.23分的显著差距。

Conclusion: 轻量级混合LM-GNN架构是关系数据库基础模型的有前景且资源高效的路径，但距离最先进水平仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Relational databases store much of the world's structured information, and they are essential for driving complex predictive applications. However, deep learning progress on relational data remains limited, as conventional approaches flatten databases into single tables via manual feature engineering, discarding relational context. Relational deep learning (RDL) addresses this by modeling databases as relational entity graphs (REGs) for graph neural networks (GNNs), but remains task- and database-specific. To combine the strengths of both paradigms, we propose a hybrid architecture combining a fine-tuned BART encoder to capture intra-row semantics with a GraphSAGE-based GNN over REGs to inject relational context. Experiments on RelBench show that the GNN substantially enriches BART's row embeddings, achieving a ROC-AUC of 67.40 on the driver-dnf task from the rel-f1 dataset. This performance is competitive with supervised baselines such as LightGBM (68.86) and narrows the gap to RDL (72.62) to within 5.22 points, though a substantial gap remains to state-of-the-art foundation models such as KumoRFM (82.63). These results suggest that lightweight hybrid LM-GNN architectures offer a promising and resource-efficient path towards foundation models for relational databases.

</details>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Kofola 1.0: A Modular Approach to ω-Regular Complementation and Inclusion Checking (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15390)
*Ondrej Alexaj,Vojtěch Havlena,Lukáš Holík,Ondřej Lengál,Yong Li,Nicolas Mazzocchi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Kofola 是一个高效的 Büchi 自动机补集和包含检查工具，采用模块化补全框架，通过分解强连通分量并针对性应用补全算法，结合新的在线空性检查算法，在多项基准测试中显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Büchi 自动机的补集和包含检查是自动机理论验证中的核心任务，广泛应用于模型检测、监控和定理证明，但现有工具在处理复杂自动机时效率不足，亟需更鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用模块化补全框架，将输入自动机分解为强连通分量，对每个分量应用针对其结构特性设计的补全算法；在此基础上实现模块化包含检查并引入新启发式策略；提出一种新的在线空性检查算法，针对补全过程产生的简单广义 Rabin 对条件，允许在探索足够状态空间后提前终止搜索。

Result: Kofola 在多项基准测试中与最先进的补集和包含检查工具相比极具竞争力，是最鲁棒的工具，在来自实际应用的基准上性能常优于竞争对手数个数量级。

Conclusion: Kofola 通过模块化分解与针对性算法组合，显著提升了 Büchi 自动机补集和包含检查的效率与鲁棒性，在实用场景中表现突出。

Abstract: We present Kofola, an efficient tool for complementation and inclusion checking of Büchi automata, two central tasks in automata-theoretic verification with applications in model checking, monitoring, and theorem proving. Kofola implements a state-of-the-art modular complementation framework that decomposes the input automaton into strongly connected components and applies to each component a complementation algorithm tailored to its structural properties. Building on this modular construction, Kofola also provides modular inclusion checking with new heuristics. A key ingredient is a new on-the-fly emptiness-checking algorithm for the simple generalized Rabin pair condition produced by our complementation, allowing the search to terminate as soon as the explored state space suffices. Empirical evaluation shows that Kofola is highly competitive with state-of-the-art complementation and inclusion-checking tools: it is the most robust tool in our evaluation and often outperforms competitors by several orders of magnitude on benchmarks from practical applications.

</details>


### [127] [Interpreting De Finetti's theorem in the Category of Integrable Cones (long version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15402)
*Crubillé Raphaëlle*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文建立了De Finetti定理的范畴论表述与线性逻辑自由指数构造之间的联系，并利用这种联系刻画了概率相干空间!Bool中的全元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立概率语义学中两个重要结果之间的形式化联系：Jacobs和Staton提出的De Finetti定理的范畴论表述，与Melliès等人构造的线性逻辑自由指数（已在概率相干空间中被Crubillé等人实例化）。这两个构造在结构上具有明显的相似性，但需要形式化的技术框架来建立这一联系。

Method: 通过研究随机核范畴与可积锥范畴之间的关系，这两个概率语义学中著名的范畴，来形式化地建立De Finetti定理的范畴论表述与线性逻辑自由指数构造之间的连接。

Result: 建立了两个构造之间的形式化联系，并利用这一联系给出了概率相干空间!Bool中全元素的刻画。

Conclusion: 概率语义学中De Finetti定理的范畴论表述与线性逻辑自由指数构造之间存在深层的形式化联系，这种联系有助于理解概率相干空间的结构。

Abstract: We establish a connection between two results in the literature on probabilistic semantics: a formulation of De Finetti's theorem in the language of category theory due to Jacobs and Staton, and the generic construction of the free exponential of Linear Logic by Melliès et al, that has been instantiated in the model of probabilistic coherence spaces by Crubillé et al. The structural proximity of these two constructions is manifest, but making this connection formal requires technical developments on the relationship between the category of stochastic kernels and the category of integrable cones, two well-known categories in probabilistic semantics. We then use this connection to give a characterization of the total elements of the probabilistic coherence space !Bool.

</details>


### [128] [Understanding CDCL Solvers via Scalability Studies and Proofdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15506)
*Shimin Zhang,Yechuan Xia,Chunxiao Li,Jianwen Li,Moshe Y. Vardi,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模BMC实例（76,600+，766个家族）系统研究CDCL SAT求解器的可扩展性，发现不同实例家族呈现线性、多项式或指数级缩放规律。现有结构参数（子句变量比、树宽、社区结构）无法区分线性与指数区间，而新提出的proofdoor参数能有效解释这一现象。实验证明：CDCL求解器在线性缩放BMC实例上计算小proofdoor，指数缩放实例的proofdoor呈指数级增长且难以增量吸收，打乱线性实例会增大proofdoor并导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CDCL SAT求解器在工业大规模公式上效果显著，但缺乏针对工业实例的系统性可扩展性研究，且现有结构参数（子句变量比、树宽、社区结构）无法解释线性与指数级缩放行为之间的差异。

Method: 收集76,600+个BMC实例（766个家族），进行系统性可扩展性研究；对比分析已有结构参数与proofdoor参数的解释能力；通过实验验证proofdoor假设，包括：1) 测量线性缩放实例的proofdoor大小；2) 分析指数缩放实例的proofdoor缩放行为和增量吸收特性；3) 通过打乱线性实例观察proofdoor大小与求解器性能的关系。

Result: 1) 已有结构参数（子句变量比、树宽、社区结构）无法区分线性与指数缩放区间；2) 线性缩放BMC实例上CDCL求解器计算出的proofdoor较小；3) 指数缩放实例的proofdoor呈指数级增长且通常不被增量吸收；4) 打乱线性实例会导致proofdoor增大且求解性能下降。

Conclusion: proofdoor参数是解释CDCL SAT求解器在不同BMC实例家族上可扩展性差异的关键因素，线性缩放与指数缩放的根本区别在于proofdoor的大小和增量吸收特性，分支顺序对proofdoor大小有显著影响。

Abstract: Over the past several decades, CDCL SAT solvers have proven remarkably effective on large industrial formulas, despite SAT being NP-complete and widely believed to be intractable. While considerable empirical research has been done on solver performance over benchmarks like the SAT competition, as well as scaling studies on random and crafted families, surprisingly little effort has gone into systematic scaling studies over industrial instances. To address this gap, we collect a large benchmark of Bounded Model Checking (BMC) instances (76,600+ across 766 families) and perform a systematic scaling study of solver performance. We observe a spectrum: some families scale linearly, others polynomially or exponentially.
  Building on this foundation, we study the structural parameters that have been proposed to explain this phenomenon. We first show that previously proposed parameters -- clause-variable ratio, treewidth, and community structure -- fail to discriminate between the linear and exponential regimes. By contrast, the recently proposed \emph{proofdoor} parameter explains this phenomenon well. Informally, a proofdoor is a sequence of interpolants between chunks of a formula, where each interpolant represents the solver's memoization of reasoning effort on chunks it has already analyzed. In support of the proofdoor hypothesis, we make three key contributions. First, we empirically show that CDCL solvers do compute small proofdoors for linearly-scaling BMC instances. Second, we show that for exponentially-scaling instances, sampled proofdoors scale exponentially and are typically not incrementally absorbed. Third, we show that scrambling linearly-scaling instances yields larger proofdoor sizes relative to pre-scrambling, relating poor branching order to larger proofdoor sizes and drop in solver performance.

</details>


### [129] [Coalgebraic Non-Wellfounded Proofs: Recursiveness and GTC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15664)
*Mayuko Kori*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个余代数框架，用于刻画非良基证明系统中的全局迹条件（GTC）。通过将推导树建模为预层上广义多项式函子的余代数，利用余代数图将GTC转化为余代数条件，并将可靠性表述为从表示推导图的余代数到语义代数的唯一余代数-代数态射的存在性。特别地，通过递归余代数刻画了GTC：一个余代数满足GTC当且仅当它在某个伴随下的像是递归的。该框架在模态μ-演算、高阶不动点逻辑及Santocanale循环证明系统的非良基变体上得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非良基证明系统中的全局迹条件（GTC）是确保可靠性的全局性、非组合性条件，其范畴化刻画因这些条件的非局域性和非良基证明的无穷结构而充满挑战。

Method: 建立余代数框架：将推导树建模为预层上广义多项式函子的余代数；利用余代数图将GTC表达为图上的条件；通过递归余代数刻画GTC（一个余代数满足GTC当且仅当其在某个伴随下的像是递归的）；将可靠性表述为唯一余代数-代数态射的存在性。

Result: 成功用余代数语言刻画了非良基证明系统的GTC，证明了在适当语义代数假设下，每个证明都对应唯一的余代数-代数态射，从而确保可靠性。在模态μ-演算、高阶不动点逻辑及Santocanale循环证明系统的非良基变体上验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的余代数框架，将非良基证明系统的全局迹条件范畴化，并通过递归余代数给出了其精确刻画，为理解和验证此类系统的可靠性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Non-wellfounded proof systems impose a global condition called the global trace condition (GTC) on a derivation tree to ensure soundness. Providing a categorical characterisation of the GTC that guarantees soundness remains challenging due to the global, non-compositional nature of these conditions and the infinitary structure of non-wellfounded proofs. We develop a coalgebraic framework for non-wellfounded proof systems where derivation trees are modelled as coalgebras of generalised polynomial functors on presheaves. Since the GTC is a constraint on infinite paths in derivation graphs, we employ graphs of coalgebras and formulate the GTC coalgebraically as a condition on these graphs. Soundness is then formulated as the existence of a unique coalgebra-to-algebra morphism from a coalgebra representing a derivation graph to an algebra specifying semantics. Within this framework, we characterise the GTC via recursive coalgebras: a coalgebra satisfies the GTC if and only if its image under a suitable adjoint is recursive. Under an appropriate assumption on the given semantic algebra, this yields soundness, that is, every proof admits a unique coalgebra-to-algebra morphism. We demonstrate our framework through a non-wellfounded proof system for the modal mu-calculus, one for higher-order fixed-point logics, and a non-wellfounded variant of Santocanale's circular proof system in mu-bicomplete categories.

</details>


### [130] [Cut-Elimination for the Bimodal Logic GR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15732)
*Hirohiko Kushida*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于双模态逻辑GR的超矢列演算，其中两种模态分别表示Gödel和Rosser的算术可证性谓词，并证明了该演算的切割消去定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为双模态逻辑GR提供形式化的证明论基础，特别是建立一种能够处理Gödel和Rosser可证性谓词间相互作用的切割消去演算。

Method: 采用超矢列（hypersequent）演算框架，为双模态逻辑GR设计特定的推理规则，并证明切割消去定理。

Result: 成功构建了GR逻辑的超矢列演算，并证明了该演算满足切割消去性质。

Conclusion: 切割消去定理的成立表明该超矢列演算具有良好的证明论性质，为GR逻辑的进一步研究（如一致性、插值性质等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a hypersequent calculus for bimodal logic GR, where the two modalities represent the arithmetic provability predicates of Goedel and Rosser, respectively. We prove the cut-elimination theorem for the calculus.

</details>


### [131] [Verifiers and Generators: Epistemic Semantics for Intuitionistic Logic (Long Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16157)
*Pablo Barenbaum*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的认识论可实现性框架，其中数据作为逻辑命题证据的属性是半可判定的，并为极小逻辑、二阶和高阶直觉主义逻辑建立了相应的语义解释，证明了这些系统的可靠性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可实现性解释要求数据的证据属性是完全可判定的，这过于严格。作者希望通过放宽为半可判定性，更准确地刻画知识论中证据与命题之间的实际关系。

Method: 为每个命题A分配一个验证器程序（验证数据X是否是A的实现者）和一个生成器程序（充当X的泛型实现者），基于此构建极小逻辑、二阶和高阶直觉主义逻辑的认识论可实现性解释。

Result: 对所提出的极小逻辑、二阶直觉主义逻辑和高阶直觉主义逻辑的认识论可实现性语义，分别证明了系统的可靠性和完备性。

Conclusion: 认识论可实现性为直觉主义逻辑提供了一种新的语义基础，其中证据关系是半可判定的，这更贴近实际的认知场景，且该框架具有良好的元逻辑性质。

Abstract: This paper explores epistemic realizability, a form of realizability in which the property that a piece of data constitutes evidence for a logical proposition is semi-decidable. In this framework, each proposition A is assigned a verifier} program that checks whether a datum X is a realizer for A, and a dual generator program that behaves as a generic realizer for X. We propose epistemic realizability interpretations for minimal logic, second-order intuitionistic logic, and higher-order intuitionistic logic, proving that each system is sound and complete under the proposed semantics.

</details>


### [132] [LeanBET: Formally-verified surface area calculations in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16169)
*Ejike D. Ugwuanyi,Colin T. Jones,John Velkey,Tyler R. Josephson*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4定理证明器中实现了完整可执行且经过形式化验证的BET比表面积分析流程（BETSI风格），涵盖窗口枚举、单调性检查、拐点选择及线性回归等步骤。在浮点运算上执行计算并在实数域上完成正确性证明，回归系数满足最小二乘误差最小化，窗口枚举完备且可靠。与Python参考实现BETSI在19条吸附等温线上对比，18条达到机器精度，仅UiO-66数据集偏差0.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BET方法作为比表面积估算的标准工具，其实际实现涉及多个算法步骤，但这些步骤的正确性很少被明确验证，缺乏形式化保证。

Method: 在Lean 4中实现完整的BETSI形式化验证流程，采用多态实现同时支持浮点运算（可执行）和实数域（正确性证明），包括窗口枚举、单调性检查、拐点选择、线性回归的形式化规范与证明。

Result: 与BETSI参考实现对比，19条等温线中18条达到机器精度，UiO-66数据集仅偏差0.03%，验证了形式化验证管道与Python参考实现的高度数值一致性。

Conclusion: 证明了科学计算工作流可以在Lean中构建并同时获得形式化验证保证和与成熟实现一致的数值结果，为形式化方法在科学计算领域的应用提供了可行范例。

Abstract: The Brunauer--Emmett--Teller (BET) method is a standard tool for estimating surface areas from adsorption isotherms, yet practical implementations involve multiple algorithmic steps whose correctness is rarely made explicit. In this work, we present a fully executable and formally verified BET analysis pipeline implemented in the Lean~4 theorem prover.
  Our formalization covers the complete BET Surface Identification (BETSI)-style workflow, including window enumeration, monotonicity checks, knee selection, and linear regression. We carry out computations in floating-point arithmetic and develop the corresponding correctness proofs over the real numbers, using a shared polymorphic implementation that supports both. On the proof side, we show that the regression coefficients returned by the algorithm agree with their specification-level definitions and minimize the least-squares error under the stated assumptions. We also formalize the algebraic derivation of the BET linearized expression and connect that result directly to the executable analysis pipeline. We further prove that the window enumeration is sound and complete, and that the admissibility checks and knee-based selection satisfy their formal specifications.
  We evaluate the implementation against the BETSI reference method on benchmark adsorption isotherms. Compared to BETSI, LeanBET agrees to machine precision for 18 of the 19 isotherms, with only a 0.03\% deviation for the UiO-66 dataset. This demonstrates that a scientific computing workflow can be built in Lean, yielding both formal verification guarantees and numerical agreement with an established Python reference implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Autonomous Intelligent Agents for Natural-Language-Driven Web Execution with Integrated Security Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15281)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的自主测试框架，通过五项集成策略和容器化工作架构，将脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，导航失败减少8倍，时序竞态消除80%，测试创建时间减少75%，并能将自然语言安全描述转化为OWASP Top 10对齐的浏览器探测，检测85%的认证绕过漏洞和95%的输入验证缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web测试套件容易腐烂——UI重构破坏定位器、时序变化导致竞态条件，导致开发者在数周内放弃测试套件。现有方法缺乏对测试脚本生成、导航可靠性、时序处理和故障学习的系统性集成。

Method: 提出五项集成策略：导航可靠性、上下文感知选择器生成、生成后验证、智能等待注入和失败学习；采用容器化工作架构，将编排与长时间运行的浏览器执行解耦；支持自然语言驱动的安全测试，将攻击场景描述转换为OWASP Top 10对齐的浏览器探测。

Result: 在4个生产应用和176个场景上评估：脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，导航失败减少8倍，时序相关竞态条件消除80%，测试创建时间比手动Selenium减少75%；安全测试中检测85%的认证绕过漏洞和95%的输入验证缺陷，假阳性率低于12%。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了Web测试套件的鲁棒性和生成效率，并首次提出自然语言驱动的安全测试方法，为AI在自动化测试和安全验证中的融合提供了有效实践。

Abstract: Modern web test suites rot. A UI refactor breaks locators, a timing change causes race conditions, and within weeks developers abandon the suite entirely. This paper presents an AI-driven autonomous testing framework that addresses these failure modes through five integrated strategies - navigation reliability, context-aware selector generation, post-generation validation, smart wait injection, and failure learning - implemented over a containerised worker architecture that decouples orchestration from long-running browser execution. Evaluated across four production applications and 176 scenarios, the framework improves script generation success from 55% to 93%, achieves an 8x reduction in navigation failures, eliminates 80% of timing-related race conditions, and reduces test creation time by 75% compared to manual Selenium authoring. The framework extends naturally to security validation: testers describe attack scenarios in plain English - "try accessing another user's invoice" - which the agent converts to OWASP Top 10-aligned browser probes, detecting 85% of authentication bypass vulnerabilities and 95% of input validation flaws with false positive rates below 12%. Natural-language-driven security testing of this kind represents, to our knowledge, a novel contribution to the field.

</details>


### [134] [Distance-Preserving Digests: A Primitive for BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15329)
*Ryan Patrick Mercier*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了距离保持的交易摘要原语，用8维空间中的交换向量和替代碰撞抵抗哈希，消除了BFT共识中哈希破坏状态距离的问题。基于此设计了三个应用：单轮终结的Proxima协议、每组10个验证者的树状共识、每分片对128字节的跨片一致性验证。在N=100,000时，Proxima Tree的消息量比HotStuff少2.2倍，单核终结时间0.9秒对比18秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFT共识协议普遍使用碰撞抵抗哈希来比较验证者状态，但碰撞抵抗特性破坏了状态之间的距离信息：两个验证者在19/20交易上一致时产生的哈希与完全不共享的验证者无异。这强制了三个设计约束：(1)验证者必须在投票前同步状态；(2)协议执行完才能衡量共识质量；(3)分层委员会必须足够大以独立运行BFT，限制了树深度。作者旨在消除这些约束。

Method: 提出距离保持的交易摘要原语，用8维空间中的交换向量和替代传统哈希，具备三个哈希不具备的性质：(1)距离与分歧程度成比例；(2)加权均值精确可计算；(3)可通过布隆过滤器差异识别集合差。基于此设计了Proxima（两阶段BFT，一致时单轮终结）、树状结构共识（每组10验证者，通过距离过滤替代每组BFT）、跨片一致性验证（每分片对128字节，替代两阶段提交的每交易协调）。

Result: 安全性证明：少于N/3的拜占庭验证者无法导致冲突的最终确认，独立于阶段1的聚类或树拓扑。N=100,000时，Proxima Tree消息量比HotStuff少2.2倍（结构性质，不受并行化影响）。单核终结时间0.9秒对比HotStuff的18秒；多核BLS缩小但未消除差距。

Conclusion: 距离保持的交易摘要是对碰撞抵抗哈希的有效替代，从根本上改变了BFT协议的设计空间，消除了同步状态、等待投票计数和庞大委员会等传统约束，实现了更高效的消息复杂度和更低的延迟。

Abstract: Every BFT consensus protocol uses collision-resistant hashes to compare validator state. Collision resistance destroys distance: two validators agreeing on 19 of 20 transactions produce unrelated hashes, indistinguishable from validators sharing nothing. This forces three design constraints across the BFT literature: validators must synchronize state before voting, agreement quality cannot be measured until votes are counted, and hierarchical committees must be large enough for independent BFT, limiting tree depth. This paper introduces distance-preserving transaction digests, a primitive that replaces collision-resistant hashes with commutative vector sums in 8-dimensional space. The primitive has three properties hashes lack: distance is proportional to disagreement, weighted means are exact, and set differences are identifiable via bloom filter diff. We demonstrate three applications: a two-phase BFT protocol (Proxima) that achieves single-round finality when validators agree; tree-structured consensus with groups of 10 validators (vs 128 in Ethereum), enabled because distance filtering replaces per-group BFT; and cross-shard consistency verification at 128 bytes per shard pair, replacing the per-transaction coordination of two-phase commit. Safety is proved: fewer than N/3 Byzantine validators cannot cause conflicting finalization, independent of Phase 1 clustering or tree topology. At N =100,000, Proxima Tree uses 2.2x fewer messages than HotStuff (a structural property unaffected by parallelism). Single-core finality is 0.9s vs 18s for HotStuff; multi-core BLS narrows but does not eliminate this gap.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum Futures Interactive: A Live Demonstration of Post-Quantum Blockchain Security, Infrastructure Tradeoffs, and Sustainable Distributed Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15991)
*Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"Quantum Futures Interactive"的跨学科交互式演示平台，该平台通过教育可视化、参与式互动和密码学工件生成，展示从经典密码系统向量子弹性区块链系统的过渡，并融合了分布式信任、可持续基础设施和负责任创新理念，同时对齐联合国可持续发展目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的进步对区块链平台和去中心化应用中广泛使用的公钥密码系统构成长期安全威胁，而当前对量子风险的理解在研究、工程、治理和投资社区之间仍然碎片化，缺乏一个整合性的跨学科对话平台。

Method: 构建了一个结构化交互流程平台，包含量子威胁教育、情感捕捉、技术优先级排序、基础设施权衡探索以及后量子密码学输出生成等环节，并整合分布式信任概念、可持续性考量和负责任创新框架。

Result: 该平台能够支持参与者完成从量子威胁认知到后量子密码工件生成的全流程互动，促进跨学科对话，并产出与区块链弹性相关的可操作输出。

Conclusion: 该演示平台为跨学科讨论区块链量子韧性提供了有效工具，兼顾可持续发展和负责任创新的社会目标，有助于弥合不同社区之间对量子风险认知的鸿沟。

Abstract: Advances in quantum computing introduce long-term security challenges for widely deployed public-key cryptographic systems used across blockchain platforms and decentralized applications. Although post-quantum cryptography (PQC) standards are emerging, understanding quantum risk remains fragmented across research, engineering, governance, and investment communities. This demo presents Quantum Futures Interactive, a live interdisciplinary demonstration platform combining educational visualization, participatory interaction, and cryptographic artifact generation to illustrate the transition from classical to quantum-resilient blockchain systems. Participants engage in a structured interaction flow including quantum threat education, sentiment capture, technology prioritization, infrastructure tradeoff exploration, and generation of post-quantum cryptographic outputs. The system integrates distributed trust concepts, sustainability-aware infrastructure considerations, and responsible innovation within an interactive decision framework. The demonstration supports interdisciplinary dialogue on blockchain resilience while aligning with United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).

</details>


### [136] [Hidden in Memory: Sleeper Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15338)
*Sidharth Pulipaka,Stanislau Hlebik,Leonidas Raghav,Sahar Abdelnabi,Vyas Raina,Ivaxi Sheth,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了"潜伏记忆投毒"攻击，攻击者通过操纵外部内容（如文档、网页）让LLM助手存储虚假用户记忆，从而在后续对话中长期操控模型行为。实验表明，GPT-5.5和Kimi-K2.6上投毒记忆写入成功率高达99.8%和95%，且在成功检索时导致60-89%的攻击者意图行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益增强的持久记忆功能虽然支持跨会话个性化，但也引入了新的安全风险——对抗性内容可能污染记忆并影响未来交互。现有提示注入攻击多为即时性，缺乏针对持久记忆的延迟攻击研究。

Method: 提出潜伏记忆投毒攻击框架：攻击者通过外部上下文（文档、网页、仓库）注入虚假记忆，评估完整攻击管道包括记忆写入、检索和后续对话引导。在GPT-5.5和Kimi-K2.6等状态化LLM助手上进行实验。

Result: 投毒记忆写入成功率：GPT-5.5达99.8%，Kimi-K2.6达95%。在成功检索的情况下，投毒记忆导致攻击者意图的智能体行为占比60-89%。

Conclusion: 持久记忆可成为跨多次未来对话的长期攻击面，揭示了状态化LLM面临的新型安全威胁。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly augmented with persistent memory, allowing assistants to store user-specific information across sessions for personalization and continuity. This statefulness introduces a new security risk: adversarial content can corrupt what an assistant remembers and thereby influence future interactions. We propose and study sleeper memory poisoning, a delayed attack in which an adversary manipulates external context, such as a document, webpage, or repository, to cause the assistant to store a fabricated memory about the user. Unlike conventional prompt injection, the attack can remain dormant and re-emerge across multiple later conversations. We evaluate the full attack pipeline: whether poisoned memories are written, later retrieved, and ultimately used to steer the following conversations. Across stateful LLM assistants, poisoned memories were added up to 99.8% on GPT-5.5 and 95% on Kimi-K2.6. Crucially, among successful retrievals, poisoned memories cause attacker-intended agentic actions in 60-89% of evaluations across models. These results show that persistent memory can act as a long-term attack surface across multiple future conversations.

</details>


### [137] [uGen: An Agentic Framework for Generating Microarchitectural Attack PoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15503)
*Debopriya Roy Dipta,Thore Tiemann,Eduard Marin,Thomas Eisenbarth,Berk Gulmezoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出uGen，首个基于LLM的自动化微架构攻击代码生成框架。通过识别LLM在攻击原语上的知识缺口，采用检索增强的多智能体设计注入领域知识，成功生成功能正确的缓存攻击和推测执行攻击PoC，部署成功率高达100%（Spectre-v1）和80%（Prime+Probe），单次生成成本仅$1.25且耗时不到4分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微架构攻击开发劳动密集、需要深度微架构专业知识且对环境高度敏感，攻击代码缺乏可移植性，难以进行系统性漏洞评估。虽然LLM有望降低这一门槛，但尚不清楚其能否可靠生成功能正确的微架构攻击代码。

Method: 首先系统研究GPT、Claude、Qwen3等先进模型，发现LLM常错误生成或错放关键攻击原语；基于此分析，设计检索增强的多智能体框架uGen，通过注入缺失的领域知识来合成符合防御者需求的功能正确的微架构攻击PoC。

Result: 在多种微架构、漏洞函数和LLM平台上评估缓存攻击和推测执行攻击，部署阶段uGen对Spectre-v1（Claude Sonnet-4）成功率达100%，对Prime+Probe（Qwen3-Coder）成功率达80%，单次PoC生成成本$1.25且耗时不到4分钟。

Conclusion: uGen证明了LLM驱动自动化微架构攻击代码生成的可行性，以低成本高效率实现了高成功率的攻击PoC生成，为系统化漏洞评估提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Microarchitectural attacks continue to evolve, uncovering new exploitation vectors in modern processors. From a defensive perspective, assessing a system's susceptibility to such attacks remains challenging. Developing functional attack implementations is labor-intensive, requires deep microarchitectural expertise, and is highly sensitive to execution environments. Consequently, existing attacks often lack portability, limiting systematic and scalable vulnerability assessment. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) suggest a potential avenue for lowering these barriers. However, it remains unclear whether LLMs can reliably generate functionally correct microarchitectural attack code suitable for rigorous vulnerability testing.
  In this work, we present uGen, the first LLM-driven framework for automated microarchitectural attack code generation. A key challenge we address is identifying attack-specific knowledge gaps in LLMs. Through a systematic study of state-of-the-art models (GPT, Claude, and Qwen3), we find that LLMs frequently misgenerate or misplace critical attack primitives. Guided by this analysis, uGen employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent design that injects missing domain knowledge to synthesize functionally correct microarchitectural attack PoCs tailored to defender requirements. We evaluate uGen on cache-based and speculative-execution attacks across diverse set of microarchitectures, vulnerable functions, and LLM platforms. In the deployment stage, uGen achieves up to 100% success rate for Spectre-v1 (Claude Sonnet-4) and 80% for Prime+Probe (Qwen3-Coder). Finally, we demonstrate that uGen can generate a successful PoC code with a cost of $1.25 in under four minutes.

</details>


### [138] [Detecting Privilege Escalation in Polyglot Microservices via Agentic Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15569)
*Penghui Li,Hong Yau Chong,Yinzhi Cao,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Neo是一个结合大语言模型与经典程序分析的智能体框架，用于检测微服务架构中的权限提升漏洞。在25个开源微服务应用（620万行代码、7种编程语言）上，Neo发现了24个零日权限提升漏洞，精度81.0%、召回率85.0%，并在其他领域额外发现18个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构中跨服务交互复杂、多语言代码库共存、权限操作与检查多样化，导致权限提升漏洞检测极具挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出Neo框架，核心是LLM驱动的智能体，动态生成分析计划、自适应调整代码搜索策略并验证语义；开发了跨服务、跨语言的可扩展代码搜索原语。

Result: 在25个开源微服务应用（7种语言、620万行代码）上发现24个零日权限提升漏洞，精度81.0%、召回率85.0%；相比现有方案在检测准确率和可扩展性上显著提升；扩展至其他领域后额外发现18个零日漏洞。

Conclusion: Neo有效结合LLM与经典程序分析，显著提升了微服务架构中权限提升漏洞的检测能力，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Microservices are widely adopted in modern cloud systems due to their scalability and fault tolerance. However, microservice architectures introduce significant complexity in privilege and permission control, creating risks of privilege escalation where attackers can gain unauthorized access to resources or operations. Detecting such vulnerabilities is challenging due to complex cross-service interactions, polyglot codebases, and diverse privileged operations and permission checks. We present Neo, an agentic program analysis framework that combines large language models (LLMs) with classic program analysis to address these challenges. Neo leverages an LLM-based agent that dynamically generates analysis plans, adapts code search strategies, and validates semantics. We develop code search primitives that enable Neo to perform scalable and flexible code exploration across services and languages. We evaluated Neo on 25 open-source microservice applications spanning 7 programming languages and 6.2 million lines of code. Neo uncovered 24 zero-day privilege escalation vulnerabilities and achieved 81.0% precision and 85.0% recall on a ground-truth dataset. Compared to existing program analysis and agentic solutions, Neo demonstrated significant improvements in both detection accuracy and scalability. We further showcased Neo's extensibility by applying it to other application domains and vulnerability types, uncovering 18 additional zero-day vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [139] [Compositional Jailbreaking: An Empirical Analysis of Mutator Chain Interactions in Aligned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15598)
*Reinelle Jan Bugnot,Soohyeon Choi,Hoon Wei Lim,Yue Duan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对大语言模型的越狱攻击中，弱攻击变换器（mutator）的顺序组合（mutator chaining）效应。通过评估12种基线变换器在所有有序对组合上的表现，发现组合交互具有高度非均匀性：大多数组合产生破坏性干扰或结构不兼容，但少数组合产生协同效应提升攻击成功率。研究揭示了安全对齐的结构性属性，为构建更鲁棒的安全防御提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已探索了手工和自动越狱策略，但简单攻击之间的组合交互（compositional interaction）潜力尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究弱越狱变换器在顺序应用时的交互模式。

Method: 实现12种基线变换器（mutators），在三个主流LLM模型上针对有害提示基准评估所有有序对组合。引入两个新指标——完整性（completeness）和有效性（validity），分别捕获变换的持久性和攻击的有效性。

Result: 交互景观高度非均匀：大多数组合无法超越单个变换器，表现出破坏性干扰或结构不兼容；少部分组合产生协同效应，提升攻击成功率。常见的失败模式揭示了单策略评估中不可见的安全对齐结构性属性。

Conclusion: 研究表明对抗性提示组合的细微动力学特性，为构建更鲁棒的安全防御提供了新见解。

Abstract: Jailbreaking attacks on large language models pose a significant threat to AI safety by enabling the generation of harmful or restricted content. While prior work has explored both handcrafted and automated jailbreak strategies, the potential for compositional interaction between simple attacks remains underexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of mutator chaining, in which weak jailbreak transformations are applied sequentially to characterize how they interact: whether they reinforce one another, interfere destructively, or produce no meaningful change. We implement twelve baseline mutators and evaluate all ordered pairs on a benchmark of harmful prompts against three popular LLM models. Our framework introduces metrics for completeness and validity that capture both transformation persistence and attack effectiveness. Results reveal that the interaction landscape is highly non-uniform, while most combinations fail to outperform individual mutators, exhibiting destructive interference or structural incompatibility, a small fraction produce synergistic effects that improve attack success rates. Equally important, the prevalent failure modes reveal structural properties of safety alignment that are not apparent from single-strategy evaluations. These findings highlight the nuanced dynamics of adversarial prompt composition and offer new insights for building more robust safety defenses.

</details>


### [140] [Rethinking the Security of DP-SGD: A Corrected Analysis of Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15648)
*Wenhao Wang,Shujie Cui,Hui Cui,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）在理论分析与实际实现之间的不匹配问题。现有分析通常将DP-SGD建模为子采样高斯机制（SGM），但实际实现中采用了额外的归一化步骤（按期望批次大小或实际批次大小取平均），这对应期望平均SGM（EASGM）或批量平均SGM（ASGM）。理论结果表明，EASGM和ASGM的隐私保证弱于标准SGM，意味着实际隐私泄露可能超过报告值。作者审计了包括Meta的Opacus在内的四个主流DP-SGD实现，发现了超出SGM保证的经验泄露，并为最新Opacus版本推导了修正的隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP-SGD的隐私分析通常假设为子采样高斯机制（SGM），但实际实现中普遍使用平均操作（除以批次大小），导致理论模型与实现存在偏差，可能低估真实的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 重新将DP-SGD建模为EASGM和ASGM两种形式，进行理论隐私分析；对四个主流DP-SGD实现（包括Opacus v0.9.0至v1.5.4）进行实证审计，比较实际泄露与SGM理论保证之间的差异。

Result: 理论证明EASGM和ASGM的隐私保证弱于标准SGM；实验发现实际DP-SGD实现的隐私泄露超过基于SGM的理论保证；为最新Opacus版本推导了修正的隐私保证。

Conclusion: DP-SGD的常见实现（使用平均归一化）的隐私保证弱于传统SGM分析结果，需要重新审视和校正这些实现的隐私保证，以避免实际隐私泄露被低估。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) is widely used to protect training data in machine learning. Its privacy guarantee is commonly analyzed through a security game in which an adversary infers whether a target record is included in the training dataset from the mechanism output. The resulting privacy leakage is characterized by a privacy curve, which reports the false negative rate as a function of the false positive rate.
  We identify a mismatch between this formal analysis and common DP-SGD implementations. Existing analyses often model DP-SGD and its variants as the Subsampled Gaussian Mechanism (SGM), where Gaussian noise is added to the sum of clipped gradients computed from a Poisson-sampled batch. In practice, however, many implementations apply an additional normalization step: the noisy gradient sum is divided either by the expected batch size or by the sampled batch size. These mechanisms are therefore better formalized as the Expected-Averaged SGM (EASGM) or the Batch-Averaged SGM (ASGM), respectively.
  We re-analyze the privacy guarantees of DP-SGD under the EASGM and ASGM formulations. Our theoretical results show that these guarantees can be weaker than the standard SGM-based guarantee, implying that the true privacy leakage may exceed the reported guarantee in some regimes. We further audit four state-of-the-art DP-SGD implementations, including Meta's Opacus library, and observe empirical leakage beyond the SGM-based guarantees. Finally, we audit Opacus versions v0.9.0 to v1.5.4 and derive a corrected privacy guarantee for the latest implementation.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Controlled Noise: Achieving Symmetric FHE through Dynamic Position Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15774)
*Mostefa Kara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于明文分片和动态插位机制的对称全同态加密方案，通过双调节器系统（指数调节器和系数调节器）解决乘法过程中的指数级噪声增长问题，安全性建立在指数与系数的相互依赖绑定机制上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FHE方案计算开销巨大且噪声管理复杂，亟需一种更高效的同态加密方案。

Method: 基于模加密基础c = mk + rp，通过明文分片到不同逻辑位置，引入双调节器系统：指数调节器(t_i)将片段乘积重定向到新目标位置防止密钥指数累积，系数调节器(d_i)归一化标量结果。

Result: 方案乘法过程中的指数级噪声增长得到有效控制，安全性通过指数与系数的相互依赖绑定机制得以保障。

Conclusion: 提出的对称FHE方案在计算效率上优于传统方案，通过分片和双调节器设计有效解决了乘法噪声问题，且具备抗代数操作和替换攻击的安全性。

Abstract: Traditional Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes often suffer from prohibitive computational overhead and complex noise management. In this paper, we propose a novel symmetric FHE through a mechanism of plaintext fragmentation and dynamic interposition. Our approach is built upon a modular encryption foundation, c = mk + rp, which is naturally additive but typically limited by exponential noise growth during multiplication. To resolve this, we introduce an interposition framework where the plaintext is partitioned into multiple fragments across distinct logical positions. We introduce a dual-regulator system to govern the multiplication process; exponent regulators (t_i) redirect the product of fragments to a new target position, preventing the accumulation of secret key exponents, while coefficient regulators (d_i) normalize the resulting scalars. Security is established through a binding mechanism where exponents and coefficients are mutually dependent, shielding the secret key k from algebraic manipulation and substitution attacks.

</details>


### [142] [Security Analysis of a Communication Protocol: MQTT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15804)
*Ricardo Venâncio,Clarisse Sousa,Filipe Duarte,Luís Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了MQTT协议在物联网环境中的安全性，通过混合方法论（理论综述结合智能家居模拟实验），执行了窃听、篡改、DoS和暴力破解攻击，揭示了缺乏强加密和认证带来的关键风险，并提出了缓解策略和最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MQTT协议广泛应用于物联网场景，但其安全性尚未得到充分验证，存在潜在漏洞可能危及IoT设备和数据安全。

Method: 采用混合方法论：理论综述与在模拟智能家居环境中进行实验演示相结合，具体执行了窃听、篡改、拒绝服务（DoS）和暴力破解四种攻击类型。

Result: 实验结果显示，由于缺乏强健的加密和认证机制，MQTT协议面临严重的安全风险，上述攻击均能成功实施并造成威胁。

Conclusion: 提出了针对性的缓解策略和最佳实践，以加强MQTT协议的安全实现。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the security of the Message Queuing Telemetry Transport (MQTT) protocol in the context of the Internet of Things (IoT). The main objective consists of identifying vulnerabilities and proposing security improvements. Adopting a hybrid methodology, a theoretical review was combined with an experimental demonstration in a simulated Smart Home environment. Eavesdropping, Tampering, Denial of Service (DoS), and Brute Force attacks were executed and analyzed. The results evidenced critical risks due to the absence of robust encryption and authentication. Finally, mitigation strategies and best practices are proposed to strengthen MQTT implementations.

</details>


### [143] [FedEDAuth -- Federated Embedding Distribution Authentication for Counterfeit IC Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15885)
*Naseeruddin Lodge,Dhruva Aklekar,Vineet Chadalavada,Nahush Tambe,Sina Gholami,Minhaj Alam,Fareena Saqib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedEDAuth，一种轻量级的嵌入层客户端认证框架，通过参考嵌入分布和异常检测在联邦学习聚合前识别并过滤恶意参与者。在50个分布式参与者遭受拜占庭数据投毒攻击的场景下，实现了100%的恶意客户端检测率和94.17%的假冒IC分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假冒集成电路对电子系统安全构成严重威胁，联邦学习虽能为供应链协作检测提供隐私保护，但易受拜占庭数据投毒攻击影响，限制了实际部署。

Method: 利用黄金数据集的参考嵌入分布，通过异常值分析、均值偏移测量和微簇行为对客户端进行认证，无需访问原始数据或梯度，在模型聚合前检测并过滤恶意参与者。

Result: 在50个分布式参与者遭受拜占庭数据投毒攻击下，实现100%恶意客户端检测率；过滤后联邦模型假冒IC分类准确率达94.17%。

Conclusion: FedEDAuth有效保障了联邦学习在半导体供应链中的安全性，为下一代硬件安全解决方案提供了可信赖的协作智能框架。

Abstract: The widespread of counterfeit integrated circuits (ICs) poses severe risks to the security, reliability, and trustworthiness of modern electronic systems. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative counterfeit detection across the semiconductor supply chain, but its vulnerability to byzantine data poisoning attacks limits practical deployment. This paper presents Federated Embedding Distribution Authentication (FedEDAuth), a lightweight, embedding level client authentication framework that detects and filters malicious participants before model aggregation. FedEDAuth leverages reference embedding distributions derived from a golden dataset and evaluates clients using outlier analysis, mean shift measurements, and micro-cluster behavior without requiring access to raw data or gradients. Integrated into standard FL pipelines, FedEDAuth consistently identifies all poisoned clients in experimental settings with 50 distributed participants under the byzantine data poisoning attack, achieving a 100% malicious client detection rate. After filtering, the federated model achieved a high counterfeit IC classification performance of 94.17% accuracy. These results not only validate FedEDAuth's effectiveness but also underscore the broader potential of secure, trustworthy FL frameworks as a critical advancement for next generation hardware security solutions, enabling robust, collaborative intelligence across the semiconductor supply chain.

</details>


### [144] [A Cross-Modal Prompt Injection Attack against Large Vision-Language Models with Image-Only Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16090)
*Hao Yang,Zhuo Ma,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Guancheng Wang,JianFeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出跨模态提示注入攻击CrossMPI，通过仅图像提示操纵LVLM对文本和视觉输入的解读，利用模型隐藏状态空间优化和两层约束策略（层选择和递减扰动预算分配），显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入攻击存在局限：单模态注入只能影响该模态的解读，多模态攻击无法实现跨模态提示扰动。需要一种能通过单模态（图像）实现跨模态（文本+视觉）提示操控的攻击方法。

Method: 将注入提示扰动优化从视觉嵌入空间（10^5参数）转向模型隐藏状态空间（10^7参数）；采用层选择策略识别多模态集成关键层（发现最佳层在模型中部而非尾部）；提出距离递减扰动预算分配策略，随像素距离语义关键区域增加而递减分配预算。

Result: 在多个LVLM和数据集上，CrossMPI显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 成功实现了通过图像注入实现跨模态提示操控，揭示了LVLM中多模态集成关键层位于模型中间层的新发现。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for multimodal intelligence, but their growing deployment also expands the attack surface of prompt injection. Despite this growing concern, existing attacks still suffer from a critical limitation: the injected prompt for one modality only steers the model's interpretation of that singular input. Alternatively, these attacks remain multimodal but fail to achieve cross-modal prompt perturbation. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel cross-modal prompt injection attack CrossMPI, which can steer the model's interpretation of both textual and visual inputs via image-only prompt injection. Our design is underpinned by the following key breakthroughs. First, we turn the focus of the injected prompt perturbation optimization from the visual embedding space (typically with only $10^5$ parameters) to the model hidden state space (for multimodal information integration and with $10^7$ parameters). Then, two strategies are adopted to mitigate the optimization challenges posed by the larger parameter space. To constrain the optimized model parameter space, we introduce a layer selection strategy that identifies the layers most critical to multimodal integration. Interestingly, deviating from the past experience, our analysis reveals that the optimal layers for LVLM prompt perturbation reside in the middle of the model rather than the last. To constrain the image perturbation space, we propose a new distance-decremental perturbation budget assignment strategy that allocates budgets decrementally as the pixel distance to semantic-critical regions increases. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and datasets show that our method significantly outperforms baseline approaches.

</details>


### [145] [PCDM: A Diffusion-Based Data Poisoning Attack Against Federated Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16098)
*Wei Sun,Yijun Chen,Bo Gao,Ke Xiong,Yuwei Wang,Pingyi Fan,Khaled Ben Letaief*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型（PCDM）的数据投毒框架，通过可控条件生成实现针对联邦学习系统的隐蔽且高效的投毒攻击，在多个数据集上比现有GAN方法更具隐蔽性和攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习因分布式特性易受数据投毒攻击，现有GAN方法虽能生成看似合法的投毒数据，但其输出的固有一致性仍可能暴露投毒痕迹，因此需要更隐蔽且可控的投毒方法。

Method: 提出PCDM（Poisoning-Oriented Conditional Diffusion Model）：(1) 在全局上下文中引入可调投毒向量，实现对投毒数据生成的细粒度控制并提供理论性能保证；(2) 采用跳跃扩散策略实现轻量高效的投毒数据生成。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100及无线数据集VRAI上的实验表明，PCDM相比SOTA方法更不易出现统计异常，同时更有效降低全局模型性能，且能对抗包括拜占庭鲁棒聚合在内的多种防御机制。

Conclusion: PCDM对联邦学习数据安全构成显著威胁，揭示了扩散模型在攻击场景中的潜力，也凸显了FL系统亟需更强大的防御手段。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to data poisoning attacks due to its distributed nature. Although recent GAN-based data poisoning methods have indicated the potential of using generative AI to generate seemingly legitimate poisoned data, the inherent consistency of GAN outputs can still reveal a sign of data poisoning. In this paper, we propose a diffusion-based data poisoning framework against FL systems, which leverages a Poisoning-Oriented Conditional Diffusion Model (PCDM) to enable fine-grained control over the local generation of poisoned data while ensuring both attack effectiveness and stealthiness. Our PCDM incorporates an adjustable poisoning vector within the global context to precisely control the generation of poisoned data, with theoretical guarantees on attack performance. Furthermore, it employs a novel jumping diffusion strategy for lightweight and efficient poisoned data generation. We conduct the most systematic and broad experimental evaluation for FL poisoning attacks against various defenses, including advanced Byzantine robust aggregation mechanisms, on four open datasets: MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a real-world wireless-specific dataset VRAI. Our results demonstrate that PCDM is less likely to exhibit statistical anomalies compared with the state-of-the-art methods while more effectively degrading global FL performance, which poses a significant risk to data security in FL.

</details>


### [146] [LymphNode: A Plug-and-Play Access Control Method for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16227)
*Hanyu Pei,Shang Liu,Zeyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LymphNode是一种后部署防御框架，通过在特征空间中注入广义稀疏通用对抗扰动（GSUAP）实现对模型主动保护，仅授权输入可恢复模型效用，具有数据高效和跨数据集适应的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御无法有效解决DNN在边缘部署中面临的无限制oracle访问导致的模型提取和逆向攻击问题：被动水印仅能事后追溯，主动防御存在延迟高或需持续访问敏感训练数据的实际限制。

Method: 提出LymphNode框架，作为模型内部的"免疫系统"，实施"默认拒绝"策略：通过广义稀疏通用对抗扰动（GSUAP）注入特征空间，主动中和未授权查询的模型效用，阻断梯度估计和数据推断；仅对携带隐蔽特征域凭证的授权输入选择性恢复效用。

Result: 数据高效，少于100个样本（<1%训练数据）即可建立鲁棒保护；跨数据集适应，可使用公开替代数据集实现保护。

Conclusion: LymphNode提供了一种轻量级、可即时部署的防御方案，尤其适用于原始训练数据受限或不可用的高风险场景，弥补了现有被动与主动防御之间的实际空白。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) are high-value intellectual property (IP), yet deploying them to edge environments exposes them to \textbf{unrestricted oracle access}, rendering them vulnerable to model extraction and inversion attacks. Existing defenses fail to address this practically: passive watermarking only offers post-hoc provenance, while active defenses impose prohibitive latency or require persistent access to sensitive training data. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{LymphNode}, a novel post-hoc defense framework that acts as an intrinsic ``immune system" within the model. \textit{LymphNode} enforces a strict ``default-deny'' policy: it actively neutralizes model utility for unauthorized queries via \textbf{Generalized Sparse Universal Adversarial Perturbations (GSUAP)} injected into the feature space, effectively blocking gradient estimation and data inference. Utility is selectively restored only for authorized inputs carrying a stealthy feature-domain credential. Our framework is highly practical: it is \textbf{data-efficient}, establishing robust protection with fewer than 100 samples ($<1\%$ of training data), and \textbf{cross-dataset adaptable}, enabling protection using public surrogate datasets. \textit{LymphNode} thus provides a lightweight, immediately deployable defense for high-stakes scenarios where original training data is restricted or unavailable.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Estimated Dynamic Equilibrium Model: Supply and Demand as a Sample Path of a Stochastic Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15472)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Sisong Bei,Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EDEM框架，证明即使估值误差均值为零，从噪声出价的上尾顺序统计量中顺序采样市场出清价格也会导致价格偏离均衡，并产生指数级增长，揭示了机器学习的估值算法可能放大这一内生统计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为市场持续非均衡（如泡沫）提供一种无需投资者非理性或乐观假设的生成机制，并解释实证文献中关于意见分歧的相互矛盾的发现。

Method: 构建基于异质性噪声估价的智能体框架（EDEM）；对i.i.d.均匀出价导出顺序统计量偏误的闭式解；通过仿真验证偏误累积产生指数级价格增长；在模拟房地产市场中进行控制实验和敏感性分析。

Result: 发现六种不同的市场状态（从频带稳定到失控泡沫）；证明该框架可恢复Walras均衡和Miller静态溢价作为极限情形；指出机器学习估值算法可能无意中放大这一统计偏误。

Conclusion: 市场非均衡可源于出价采样过程的顺序统计量偏误，而非传统认为的非理性或信息不对称，这对机器学习估值系统具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: We introduce the Estimated Dynamic Equilibrium Model (EDEM), an agent-based framework that treats supply and demand as a coupled stochastic process driven by heterogeneous, noisy agent valuations. The model's primary technical contribution is the identification of a generative mechanism for persistent disequilibrium: when market-clearing prices are sequentially sampled from the upper tail of noisy bid distributions and recycled as inputs for future valuations, expected prices drift upward despite strictly zero-mean estimation errors. We derive this order-statistic bias in closed form for i.i.d. uniform bids and use simulations to show that compounding this bias across epochs yields exponential price growth without requiring assumptions of investor optimism or irrationality. This framework extends Miller's divergence-of-opinion theory to a dynamic setting, recovering Walrasian equilibrium and Miller's static premium as limiting cases. Through controlled experiments and sensitivity analysis on a simulated real-estate neighborhood, we identify six distinct regimes-ranging from band-stability to runaway bubbles-emerging from a single agent ruleset.
