These results offer a potential explanation for the contradictory findings in the empirical divergence-of-opinion literature and suggest that machine-learning valuation algorithms may inadvertently amplify this inherent statistical bias.

</details>


### [148] [Multi-Agent Cooperative Transportation: Optimal and Efficient Task Allocation and Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16097)
*Ning Zhou,Nikolai W. F. Bode,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CT-TAPF问题，形式化了需要多机器人协作搬运大件物品的路径规划与任务分配问题，并设计了最优求解器CT-TCBS（含增量扩展策略）以及一系列次优求解器（基于全局任务视角），实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF和TAPF框架缺乏多机器人真正协作搬运大件物品的能力，存在明显的空白。

Method: 形式上定义CT-TAPF问题，提出最优求解器CT-TCBS及增量扩展策略缓解组合爆炸；采用全局任务视角构建次优求解器（Best Task / Worst Task策略）。

Result: (1)增量扩展策略显著优于朴素组合方法；(2)发现任务-冲突扩展困境，强冲突解析器在集成环境中可能适得其反；(3)次优求解器在解质量与运行时间权衡上建立了更高效的边界。

Conclusion: 为需要多机器人协作的新一类实际问题提供了基础框架和有效算法集。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are integral to modern logistics, but their capabilities are often limited to tasks executable by individual agents. This paper addresses a critical gap in existing frameworks like Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) and Task Allocation and Path Finding (TAPF), which lack true cooperation for transporting large items that require multiple agents. To this end, we formalise the Cooperative Transportation Task Allocation and Path Finding (CT-TAPF) problem, which integrates team formation, task assignment, and collision-free pathfinding. We present an optimal solver, Cooperative Transportation Task Conflict-Based Search (CT-TCBS), which features a novel Incremental Expansion strategy to tackle the combinatorial explosion inherent in team formation. Recognising the computational cost of optimality, we also develop a family of sub-optimal solvers that employ a global, task-centric perspective, selecting the next task to assign based on a global difficulty metric (Best Task or Worst Task). Our comprehensive empirical evaluation demonstrates three key findings: (1) the incremental expansion strategy significantly outperforms the naive combinatorial approach by successfully pruning the dominant task-allocation search space; (2) we identify a task-conflict expansion dilemma, where sophisticated conflict resolvers effective for large-agent pathfinding subproblems can be detrimental in the integrated CT-TAPF setting; and (3) our proposed sub-optimal solvers establish a new, more efficient frontier on the solution quality-runtime spectrum compared to "nn-" agent-centric baselines. This work provides a foundational framework and a set of effective algorithms for a new, practical class of cooperative multi-agent problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Bridging Silicon and the Hippocampus: Algebro-Deterministic Memory "VaCoAl" as a Substrate for Vector-HaSH and TEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15652)
*Hiroyuki Chuma,Kanji Otsuka,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出VaCoAl——一种基于伽罗瓦域LFSR的代數确定性超维记忆架构，作为统一网格-海马回放与多跳衰减的代数对象，并证明其与Vector-HaSH/TEM框架的形式等价性、STDP路径选择机制及对EC-CA3/DG-CA3通路演化的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vector-HaSH/TEM理论与人类iEEG中SWR门控情景回忆、多跳回放保真度乘法衰减等实证发现长期平行发展，缺乏一个共享的代数对象来统一解释。VaCoAl填补了这一空白。

Method: 采用伽罗瓦域LFSR构造确定性扩散替代随机投影；定义路径积分置信比CR₂（n跳链上CR₁的乘积）作为多跳回放衰减的代数模型；通过相似性保持、组合可逆性和有界前沿搜索推导STDP样路径选择；建立与EC-CA3和EC-DG-CA3通路的形式对应。

Result: VaCoAl满足与随机投影相同的准正交性要求，具有匹配的二阶矩统计量、更强的雪崩行为和比特精确可重现性；CR₂条件独立乘积形式首次为乘法衰减提供可代数处理的模型；推导出可验证的iEEG预测。

Conclusion: VaCoAl架起了计算神经科学与超维工程之间的桥梁，为海马-内嗅环路的功能架构提供了能量-容量-可塑性统一的演化解读。

Abstract: Vector-HaSH and the Tolman-Eichenbaum Machine (TEM) propose that the hippocampal-entorhinal circuit factorizes content from a prestructured grid-cell scaffold and supports compositional memory via ripple-mediated replay. Human iEEG shows that hippocampal sharp-wave ripples (SWRs) gate episodic recall, ripple-locked cortical reactivation recapitulates encoding-time patterns, and multi-hop replay fidelity decays multiplicatively along sequence length. These literatures have advanced in parallel without a shared algebraic object. We show that VaCoAl, an algebro-deterministic hyperdimensional memory architecture built from Galois-field LFSRs, supplies that object. Specifically, deterministic Galois-field diffusion provides a substrate-level alternative to Vector-HaSH's random scaffold-to-hippocampus projection that satisfies the same quasi-orthogonality requirement, with matched second-moment statistics, stronger avalanche behavior, and bit-exact reproducibility. The path-integral Confidence Ratio $CR_2$, the product of per-step $CR_1$ values along an $n$-hop chain, is the natural functional form for multi-hop replay-fidelity decay under conditional independence of per-step reactivation, providing the first algebraically tractable model of reported multiplicative decay. STDP-like path selection in VaCoAl follows from architectural demands -- similarity preservation, compositional reversibility, and bounded-frontier search -- that also constrain hippocampal computation. We further argue that VaCoAl operating regimes share architectural commitments with the EC--CA3 and EC--DG--CA3 pathways, motivating an energy-capacity-plasticity reading of why both are conserved across $>$520 Myr of evolution and primate dentate-gyrus elaboration. We prove formal correspondences, derive testable iEEG predictions, and bridge computational neuroscience and hyperdimensional engineering.

</details>


### [150] [General-Purpose Co-Evolutionary Construction of Parallel Algorithm Portfolios for Multi-Objective Binary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15729)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Shaofeng Zhang,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DACMO，一种面向多目标二元优化问题的通用并行算法组合构建方法，通过神经实例表示架构解耦域不变与实例特定特征，并引入LLM自动生成搜索算子，在四类问题上无需修改即可超越基于经典模板的PAP，性能媲美甚至超过依赖手动设计问题特定实例生成器的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用并行算法组合（PAP）构建方法在多目标二元优化问题（MOBOP）上仍缺乏通用方案，填补这一空白。

Method: 提出DACMO，包含两项技术创新：(1) 神经实例表示架构，解耦域不变特征与实例特定特征，实现跨维度的类一致实例生成，无需问题特定的实例生成器；(2) 基于LLM的自动搜索算子生成，将搜索空间从预定义模板的参数调优扩展到算子级别的算法设计。

Result: 在MMMP、MKP、MCCP、MCIMP四类MOBOP上，DACMO无需修改即可直接应用，超越基于经典MOEA模板构建的PAP，性能与依赖手动设计问题特定实例生成器的特权基线相当，且在四类问题中的两类上超越该基线。

Conclusion: DACMO是一种有效的通用PAP构建方法，具备跨问题类的泛化能力，且通过LLM自动生成算子避免了人工设计依赖。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in constructing generalizable parallel algorithm portfolios (PAPs), no general-purpose approach is yet available for multi-objective binary optimization problems (MOBOPs). To fill this gap, this paper proposes domain-agnostic co-evolution of parameterized search for multi-objective binary optimization~(DACMO), which features two technical innovations. First, we propose a neural instance representation architecture that decouples domain-invariant and instance-specific features, enabling class-consistent instance generation across varying dimensions without problem-specific instance generators. Second, we introduce LLM-based automatic search operator generation into PAP construction, extending the search space from parameter tuning of predefined templates to operator-level algorithm design. We evaluate DACMO on four representative MOBOP classes to demonstrate its effectiveness as a general-purpose PAP construction method: the multi-objective match max problem~(MMMP), the multi-objective knapsack problem~(MKP), the multi-objective contamination control problem (MCCP), and the multi-objective complementary influence maximization problem~(MCIMP). Experimental results show that DACMO can be directly applied to all four problem classes without modification, outperforms PAPs built from classic MOEA templates, and achieves performance comparable to a privileged state-of-the-art baseline that relies on manually designed problem-specific instance generators, while outperforming it on two of the four evaluated problem classes.

</details>


### [151] [Structure Abstraction and Generalization in a Hippocampal-Entorhinal Inspired World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15733)
*Tianqiu Zhang,Muyang Lyu,Xiao Liu,Si Wu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大脑启发的分层模型，通过同时推断潜在转换和构建预测性视觉世界模型，从连续高维动态中提取抽象结构，并利用速度驱动的路径整合实现跨上下文的鲁棒预测和结构泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海马-内嗅皮层回路已知能表征空间和概念空间，但如何从连续高维动态中同时提取抽象结构的机制仍不清楚，现有模型缺乏对结构提取与预测世界模型构建的统一解释。

Method: 提出一种受大脑启发的分层模型，包含逆模型用于结构提取，以及HPC-MEC耦合模型用于分离关系结构（MEC）与整合情景场景（HPC），利用速度驱动的路径整合实现预测和结构重用。

Result: 在原始变换动力学基准上验证了模型的结构抽象能力，展示了跨不同上下文的鲁棒预测和结构泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解大脑启发的自监督世界模型学习如何获取可重用的抽象知识提供了新颖的计算框架，弥合了神经科学与机器学习之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Humans abstract experiences into structured representations to facilitate pattern inference and knowledge transfer. While the hippocampal-entorhinal (HPC-MEC) circuit is known to represent both spatial and conceptual spaces, the mechanisms for concurrently extracting abstract structures from continuous, high-dimensional dynamics remain poorly understood. We propose a brain-inspired hierarchical model that simultaneously infers latent transitions and constructs a predictive visual world model. Our architecture employs an inverse model for structural extraction alongside an HPC-MEC coupling model that dissociates relational structures (MEC) from integrated episodic scenes (HPC). Using primitive transformation dynamics as a benchmark, we demonstrate the model's capacity for structural abstraction. By leveraging velocity-driven path integration, the framework enables robust prediction and structural reuse across diverse contexts, thereby achieving structural generalization. This work provides a novel computational framework for understanding how brain-inspired, self-supervised learning of world models facilitates the acquisition of reusable abstract knowledge.

</details>


### [152] [Diversified Residual Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15809)
*Koki Ikeda,Masahiro Nomura,Ryoki Hamano*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出多样化残差符号回归（DRSR），基于质量-多样性范式生成多个在残差分布上具有差异的表达式，让用户根据领域知识选择最合理的模型，解决了符号回归对异常值敏感且需要用户参与判断的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法忽略了对异常值判断的模糊性以及用户专业知识的参与，鲁棒回归虽能降低异常值权重但无法提供多种可选方案，因此需要一种能生成多样化残差模式表达式的方法。

Method: 基于质量-多样性（Quality-Diversity）范式，在保持高预测精度的同时，显式促进表达式在残差分布模式上的多样性，从而收集多个拟合良好但残差分布不同的表达式。

Result: 在合成混合数据集上，DRSR比传统符号回归生成更多样的表达式，并能捕获多种底层关系；在真实天文数据集上，发现了符合已知物理关系的多个表达式。

Conclusion: DRSR通过提供残差模式多样化的候选表达式集合，有效支持用户根据领域知识进行后搜索选择，增强了符号回归在实际应用中处理异常值的灵活性与可解释性。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover explicit mathematical expressions that explain observed data and is widely used in domains where interpretability is essential. Because interpretability requires expressions to reflect meaningful regularities, SR is sensitive to observations that deviate from the dominant relationship. Such irregular observations, or outliers, are common in real-world data and can hinder SR from identifying underlying regularities. Robust regression mitigates this by downweighting observations with large residuals. However, deciding which observations should be treated as outliers is often ambiguous and depends on user interpretation and domain knowledge, a perspective largely overlooked in existing SR studies. This motivates approaches that present multiple candidate expressions, allowing users to examine different residual patterns and choose expressions consistent with their expertise. We propose diversified residual symbolic regression (DRSR), which achieves high predictive accuracy while promoting diversity with respect to residual patterns based on the Quality-Diversity paradigm. DRSR collects multiple expressions that fit the data well but differ in how residuals are distributed, enabling post-search selection aligned with domain knowledge. On a synthetic mixture dataset, DRSR produces more diverse expressions than conventional SR while capturing multiple underlying relationships. On a real-world astronomical dataset, DRSR discovers multiple expressions consistent with known physical relationships.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Lightweight Cross-Device Sleep Tracking on the WeBe Wearable Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15719)
*Wei Shao,Ehsan Kourkchi,Krishi Prashant Shah,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、可复现的睡眠追踪流程，直接处理原始加速度计信号，通过逐epoch活动特征、时间平滑、归一化评分和全局校准阈值实现睡眠/清醒分类。在MMASH数据集上校准，并在WeBe平台和ActiGraph设备上跨设备验证，总睡眠时间平均绝对误差为41.6分钟（MMASH）和27.4分钟（WeBe）， onset/offset误差均小于14分钟，性能优于商用ActiGraph流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备广泛用于连续健康监测，但由于依赖专有算法和设备特定活动表示，可靠睡眠追踪在新型平台上仍未被充分探索。

Method: 将原始加速度计数据转换为逐epoch活动特征，应用时间平滑和归一化评分，使用全局校准阈值进行睡眠/清醒分类。在MMASH数据集上校准，并在WeBe平台和ActiGraph设备上进行跨设备评估。

Result: MMASH上总睡眠时间平均绝对误差41.6分钟，onset和offset误差分别为6.3和7.4分钟；WeBe上TST误差27.4分钟，onset和offset误差分别为13.9和8.0分钟，优于商用ActiGraph流程。

Conclusion: 该简单可复现的流程能实现准确且泛化性良好的睡眠追踪。

Abstract: Wearable devices are widely used for continuous health monitoring, yet reliable sleep tracking on emerging platforms remains underexplored due to reliance on proprietary algorithms and device-specific activity representations. We present a lightweight and reproducible sleep tracking pipeline that operates directly on raw accelerometer signals. The method converts data into epoch-level activity features, applies temporal smoothing and normalized scoring, and performs sleep/wake classification using a globally calibrated threshold. We calibrate the model on the Multilevel Monitoring of Activity and Sleep in Healthy People (MMASH) dataset and evaluate it in a cross-device study using the WeBe wearable platform and a commercial ActiGraph device. On MMASH, the method achieves a mean absolute error of 41.6 minutes in Total Sleep Time (TST), with onset and offset errors of 6.3 and 7.4 minutes. On real-world WeBe data from three participants across five sessions, it achieves a mean TST error of 27.4 minutes and onset and offset errors of 13.9 and 8.0 minutes. In contrast, a commercial ActiGraph pipeline shows larger discrepancies relative to ground truth. These results demonstrate accurate and generalizable sleep tracking using a simple and reproducible pipeline.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Overreliance in Writing Tasks: Exploring Similarity-Based Measures of AI Influence on Writing and Proposing a Reflective Writing Interface Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15322)
*Vitor H. A. Welzel,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究47名参与者在有无AI辅助下完成写作任务的情况，量化了AI建议与用户写作的文本重叠度，发现AI辅助与建议复用模式相关。基于此设计了交互式写作界面，并通过小规模有声思维研究（n=4）初步证明该界面能提升用户对AI输出融入写作的感知，促进对AI辅助的更自觉参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注AI过度依赖问题，但在开放式写作过程中实证观察这一现象的方法仍有限。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过实证方法考察生成式AI辅助如何影响用户与AI建议的交互行为。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：(1) 47名参与者完成有无AI辅助的分析与综合写作任务，量化AI建议与写作内容的文本重叠度，并分析参与者反思；(2) 基于发现设计交互式写作界面，并通过小规模有声思维研究（n=4）评估其对用户反思能力的影响。

Result: AI辅助与建议复用模式显著相关；所设计的交互式界面能提升用户对AI输出如何融入自身写作的感知，可能促进对AI辅助的更自觉使用。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了研究AI在写作场景中采纳的实证方法，并证明界面设计能够塑造用户与AI的交互方式，为未来促进用户对AI辅助的更审慎使用提供了设计方向。

Abstract: As generative AI (GenAI) systems become increasingly proficient at simulating human-like and well-reasoned text, users may attribute authority to AI outputs, shaping how they engage with writing and reasoning tasks. While prior work has raised concerns about AI overreliance, empirical approaches for observing this phenomenon during open-ended writing remain limited. In this paper, we examine how GenAI assistance influences users' interactions with AI suggestions during writing. We report results from a mixed-methods study in which 47 participants completed analysis and synthesis writing tasks with or without AI assistance. We quantify the textual overlap between AI suggestions and participants' writing and analyze participants' reflections. Our results show that AI assistance is associated with patterns of suggestion reuse. Building on these findings, we design and evaluate an interactive writing interface that may support reflection on the usage of the AI suggestions during writing. Evidence from a small follow-up think-aloud study (n = 4) suggests that the interface can increase users' awareness of how AI outputs are incorporated into their writing and may support more conscious engagement with AI assistance. Together, our findings contribute empirical methods for studying AI adoption in writing contexts and demonstrate how interface design can shape user-AI interaction.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-Turn Neural Transparency: Surfacing Neural Activations Improves User Calibration to LLM Behavioral Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15455)
*Sheer Karny,Anthony Baez,Pat Pataranutaporn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多轮神经透明度方法，通过实时可视化LLM的内部神经激活状态，帮助用户预测和识别聊天机器人行为随对话轮次的变化。实验表明，该可视化显著提升了用户对机器人行为特质的预测和评估准确性，并降低了过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人行为往往对用户不透明，响应可能在对话中不可预测地变化，趋向谄媚、毒性或其他不安全回复，使用户面临被误导或操纵的风险。

Method: 利用机械可解释性方法构建六种人格特质的行为向量（通过对比系统提示识别激活空间中与特质表达相关的方向，R²≥0.9），并设计旭日图+漂移面板实时可视化每轮对话中的特质表达变化。通过随机对照实验（N=246）比较无可视化、静态单轮可视化和多轮动态可视化三种条件下的用户表现。

Result: 无可视化组难以准确评估特质（RMSE≈0.6-0.7）；神经透明度显著提升了预测和评估准确性（d=-0.34至-0.49）；多轮动态可视化在整体评估上优于静态单轮可视化（d=-0.32）；透明度还降低了过度自信。

Conclusion: 向日常用户展示内部模型表征是走向更透明、更知情的人机交互的有意义一步。

Abstract: Chatbot behavior is often opaque to users, as responses can shift unpredictably across a conversation, drifting toward sycophancy, toxicity, or other unsafe responses. This can leave users vulnerable, either being misled by overly agreeable AI or manipulated by a harmful chatbot that no longer behaves as intended. To address this, we introduce multi-turn neural transparency, an interface that surfaces an LLM's internal neural activations in real time to help users anticipate and recognize how behaviors change across turns. We construct behavioral vectors for six personality traits using methods from mechanistic interpretability, identifying directions in activation space that correlate with trait expression ($R^2 \geq 0.9$) via contrastive system prompts, and visualize trait expression using a sunburst and drift panel that updates at each turn. In a randomized controlled study (N = 246), participants predicted trait expression from a system prompt alone, then rated observed behavior after interacting with the chatbot for both assistant and role-play personas. We find that participants without visualization struggled to accurately evaluate traits (RMSE $\approx$ 0.6-0.7), while the inclusion of neural transparency significantly improved both anticipation and evaluation compared to no visualization (d = -0.34 to -0.49). The multi-turn dynamic visualization additionally outperformed the static single-turn visualization on holistic evaluation of model behavior (d = -0.32). Transparency also reduced overconfidence: participants without visualization grew more confident despite no gain in accuracy. These findings suggest that surfacing internal model representations to everyday users is a meaningful step toward more transparent and informed human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [156] [GreenZ: A Sustainable UX Framework for Complex Digital Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15468)
*Trisha Solanki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GreenZ 是一个三层可持续用户体验框架，包含哲学层、运营框架层和工具层，核心贡献包括数字废物分类法（8种废物类型）和AI充分性决策模型，旨在解决数字系统日益臃肿、浪费和增加用户认知负担的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字系统变得越来越强大但也越来越浪费：功能堆积无人使用、数据过度收集、AI部署以巨大能耗和水资源成本换取本可通过更简单方法实现的收益，用户则默默承受认知负荷、时间损失和信任侵蚀的后果。

Method: 提出了GreenZ三层可持续用户体验框架：1）哲学层——基于10条已发表原则；2）运营框架层——包含5个应用系统；3）工具与画布层——提供审计工具和决策模型。核心包含数字废物分类法（8种废物类型）和AI充分性决策模型（先问AI是否应该存在，再问如何实现）。

Result: GreenZ v1在理论上已建立，但尚未经过实证验证；作者在投稿时正在进行一项从业者专家评审研究。

Conclusion: 论文展示了框架的架构、概念基础、与现有文献的关系，并诚实阐述了仍需建立的内容。

Abstract: Digital systems have become simultaneously more powerful and more wasteful. Features accumulate that nobody uses. Data is collected that nobody analyzes. AI is deployed at significant energy and water costs for gains that a simpler approach could have achieved. And through all of it, the people who depend on these systems quietly absorb the consequences in cognitive load, lost time, and eroded trust. This paper introduces GreenZ, a three-layer Sustainable UX Framework for complex digital systems. Its three layers are a Philosophy Layer built around ten published principles, an Operational Frameworks Layer comprising five applied systems, and a Tools and Canvases Layer of practical audit instruments and decision models. Two contributions sit at the framework's core: a Digital Waste Taxonomy classifying eight distinct waste types, and an AI Sufficiency Decision Model that asks whether AI should exist in a given flow before any question of how to implement it. GreenZ v1 is theoretically grounded but empirically unvalidated. A practitioner expert review study is underway at the time of submission. The paper presents the framework's architecture, its conceptual foundations, its position relative to existing literature, and an honest account of what remains to be established.

</details>


### [157] [TopoClaw: A Human-Centric and Topology-Aware Agent Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15556)
*Heyuan Huang,Yeyi Guan,Jihong Wang,Mingzhi Wang,Jiamu Zhou,Xiangmou Qu,Jiaxin Yin,Xin Liao,Xingyu Lou,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TopoClaw 是一个开源、以人为本、拓扑感知的 Agent 操作系统，将用户生态系统建模为物理设备拓扑与社会关系拓扑的双层结构，实现跨设备动作放置、跨用户身份归因和跨上下文权限治理，以支持分布式、协作式、权限敏感的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent OS 以智能体为中心，将 OS 视为单主机运行时环境，未能解决自主行动如何融入分布式、协作式和权限敏感的工作流这一开放问题。

Method: 将用户生态系统建模为物理设备拓扑（异构设备集群）与社会关系拓扑（共享空间、团队、委派角色）的双层耦合结构，围绕可问责的跨边界执行，提出三项核心设计：跨设备动作放置、跨用户身份归因（智能体作为社会性数字孪生）、跨上下文权限治理。

Result: 提出了一个工程导向的参考架构，涵盖设计原则、运行时、跨设备执行、协作机制、安全模型和部署前景。

Conclusion: TopoClaw 从智能体中心转向人类中心，通过拓扑感知的 OS 层设计，在赋予智能体广泛能力的同时，通过分布式上下文感知的策略执行来约束其自主性，实现人机协作中的可问责性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have evolved AI assistants into autonomous reasoning engines that maintain context, invoke tools, and pursue long-horizon tasks. This has spurred Agent Operating Systems (Agent OS) as kernel-like layers for lifecycle management, memory, scheduling, and access control. Yet most designs remain agent-centric, treating the OS as a single-host runtime for internal reasoning and tool use, leaving open how autonomous actions integrate with distributed, collaborative, permission-sensitive workflows.
  TopoClaw is an open-source, human-centric, topology-aware Agent OS modeling the user's ecosystem as two coupled structures: a physical device topology of heterogeneous surfaces and a social relationship topology of shared spaces, teams, and delegated roles. It unifies device operation, messaging, and skills around accountable cross-boundary execution, with three core contributions: (1) cross-device action placement, decoupling intent from actuation and routing distributed actions across the device cluster based on hardware affordances and user context; (2) cross-user identity attribution, treating agents as socially situated "Digital Twins" that coordinate in multi-user spaces while preserving provenance, role-aware permissions, and human accountability; (3) cross-context authority governance, pairing broad capability with distributed, context-aware policy enforcement across physical and social trust boundaries to bound proactive autonomy at the OS layer.
  This report presents TopoClaw as an engineering-oriented reference architecture, covering its design principles, runtime, cross-device execution, collaboration mechanisms, security model, and deployment outlook.

</details>


### [158] [Handwriting decoding as a challenging motor task for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15698)
*Srinivas Ravishankar,Ishayu Ghosh,Nora Zajzon,Teng Fei,Virginia de Sa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出将手写解码作为脑电图基础模型的挑战性任务，发现现有基础模型在手写解码任务上表现不如专用小模型，并揭示了时间窗口混淆、信号质量提升比数据扩展更有效等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在运动想象任务上表现优异，但缺乏更具挑战性的评估任务。手写解码作为精细运动任务，能更严格地测试模型的泛化能力。同时，现有数据集存在潜在的混淆因素，需要更严谨的评估基准。

Method: 提出4字母手写解码分类任务，构建更严格的数据集，对比现有基础模型与专用小模型的性能，分析时间窗口混淆、信号质量与数据规模的影响。

Result: (a) 运动起始时间知识对性能至关重要，去掉后平均准确率从41.3%降至32.4%；(b) 提升测试信号质量带来显著提升（最佳被试从45%到78%），优于增加训练数据；(c) 现有基础模型在手写解码任务上被专用小模型超越。

Conclusion: 手写解码是评估EEG基础模型的有效挑战性基准；现有基础模型泛化能力有限；信号质量提升比数据扩展更关键；未来工作需关注时间对齐和精细运动解码。

Abstract: Recent attempts at creating Foundation Models (FMs) for Electroencephalography (EEG) have achieved state-of-the-art performance on multiple tasks including Motor Imagery (MI). These MI tasks have typically involved coarse classification between imagined limb movements. However, the development of foundation models necessitates diverse datasets, both for pretraining and evaluating the progress of these models. In this work, we propose handwriting decoding as a challenging motor task for FMs. We show that several existing datasets are potentially confounded, and introduce a dataset that more rigorously evaluates models. On this dataset, we find that current FMs, despite showing SOTA performance in multiple MI datasets are outperformed by smaller task-specific models. We also highlight challenges specific to EEG-based handwriting decoding to inform future work. In our 4-letter classification task, we show that (a) Knowledge of movement-onset is crucial to reported decoding performance in prior works, with average performance across subjects dropping from $41.3\%$ to $32.4\%$. (b) Increasing test-time signal quality provides significant performance improvements ($45\%$ to $78\%$ in our best subject) compared to scaling training data with single-trial EEG. (c) While scaling training data steadily improves decoding performance, existing FMs do not outperform specialist models in handwriting decoding. We make our code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EEG-Handwriting-BCI-DFCD/

</details>


### [159] [Separating Acute Psychological Stress from Physical Exertion in Biometric Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15756)
*Esther Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过19名受试者在静坐、行走和骑行三种活动条件下完成认知压力任务（n-back算术任务结合社会压力与经济奖励）的实验，分析了五种生理信号（皮肤电活动、斜方肌肌电、心率、心率变异性、呼吸率）对认知压力和身体活动的独立与交互反应。结果表明，强直性皮肤电活动对认知压力（r=0.48）和身体活动（r=0.67）均表现出稳健的加性效应，且无交互作用，是身体活动中检测认知压力的最优通道；心率和肌电几乎仅受身体活动驱动；心率变异性和呼吸率对认知压力敏感度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中（交通、职业场景、体育活动等）存在广泛的急性心理压力，其可靠检测可实现自适应系统响应并支持人类福祉。然而，自动压力识别面临的核心挑战是如何区分急性心理压力与并发身体活动产生的生物特征信号，现有研究对二者交互作用的系统分析不足。

Method: 采用2×3被试内设计，19名参与者在静坐、行走、骑行三种身体活动条件下，通过n-back算术任务结合社会压力与经济奖励诱导急性心理压力。采集五种生理信号：强直性皮肤电活动、斜方肌肌电、心率、心率变异性（RMSSD）和呼吸率。使用多水平线性混合模型和重复测量方差分析分解每种传感器的主效应和交互效应。

Result: 强直性皮肤电活动对认知压力（r=0.48）和身体活动（r=0.67）均表现出稳健的加性效应，无交互作用。心率和斜方肌肌电几乎仅受身体活动驱动，对认知压力无可靠敏感性。RMSSD被身体活动强烈抑制，对认知负荷仅表现出边际敏感性。呼吸率主要受身体活动支配，主分析中无可靠压力效应。

Conclusion: 提供了面向真实场景压力检测的传感器特异性层级，强调强直性皮肤电活动是在身体活动人群中识别认知压力时信息量最大的通道。

Abstract: Acute psychological stress occurs in a wide range of everyday contexts, including transportation, occupational settings, and physical activity, where its reliable detection could enable adaptive system responses and support human well-being. A persistent challenge in automated stress recognition is disentangling the biometric signatures of acute psychological stress from those of concurrent physical exertion. This study examined how five physiological signals (tonic electrodermal activity, trapezius electromyography, heart rate, heart rate variability, and respiration rate) respond to cognitive stress and physical activity, independently and in combination. Nineteen participants completed a 2x3 within-subjects design in which acute psychological stress was induced via an n-back arithmetic task combined with social pressure and financial reward, across three activity conditions: idle sitting, walking, and stationary cycling. Multilevel linear mixed models and repeated-measures ANOVA were used to decompose main effects and interactions for each sensor. Tonic electrodermal activity showed a robust, additive response to both cognitive stress (r=0.48) and physical exertion (r=0.67), with no interaction, making it the most promising candidate for stress detection during physical activity. Heart rate and trapezius electromyography were driven almost exclusively by physical exertion, with no reliable sensitivity to the stress task. RMSSD was strongly suppressed by physical activity and showed only marginal sensitivity to cognitive load. Respiration rate was dominated by physical activity, with no reliable stress effect in the primary analysis. These findings provide a sensor-specific hierarchy for real-world stress detection and highlight tonic electrodermal activity as the most informative channel when cognitive stress must be identified in physically active populations.

</details>


### [160] [Toward Natural and Companionable Virtual Agents via Cross-Temporal Emotional Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15812)
*Feier Qin,Xiao Li,Yi Zheng,Haibin Huang,Hanyao Wang,Xiaoyu Wang,Yan Lu,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出跨时间情感建模（CTEM）框架，通过将长期行为历史与瞬时情感表达相连接，使对话智能体能够实现更自然、连贯的长期陪伴交互。在即时通讯平台上实现的Auri智能体经过21天实地研究，在自然度、连贯性和情感和谐方面均表现出改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话智能体虽然取得了进展，但仍无法支持自然、长期的类陪伴交互，体验显得片段化且不真实。现有方法忽视了对智能体社交行为和内部情感的跨时间建模：生成的行为很少影响情感状态，情感状态也很少塑造后续行为。

Method: 提出跨时间情感建模（CTEM）框架，建立了一个闭环系统：过去经历更新不断演变的情感状态；该状态调节即时交互；用户反馈持续修正记忆和情感状态，从而实现反思和预期。在即时通讯平台上实现为Auri陪伴智能体。

Result: 在21天实地研究中，CTEM在感知自然度、连贯性和情感和谐方面均表现出改进。

Conclusion: 跨时间情感建模是提升对话智能体长期陪伴体验质量的有效方法，通过建立行为与情感之间的双向闭环，能够产生更真实、连贯的人机交互体验。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled conversational agents that aim for sustained companionship rather than mere task completion. Yet most still remain unable to support natural, long-term companion-like interactions, resulting in experiences that feel episodic and inauthentic. We argue that current agents overlooked cross-temporal modeling of agents' social behaviors and internal emotions: generated behaviors rarely influence an agent's emotional state, and emotional states seldom shape subsequent behaviors. We present Cross-Temporal Emotion Modeling (CTEM), a framework that links long-term behavioral history to moment-to-moment emotional expression. CTEM establishes a closed loop where past experiences update an evolving emotional state; this state conditions immediate interactions; and user feedback continually revises both memory and emotional state, enabling reflection and anticipation. We instantiate CTEM as Auri, a companion agent on an instant-messaging platform, and report a 21-day in-the-wild study showing that CTEM shows improvements in perceived naturalness, coherence, and emotional harmony.

</details>


### [161] [Which Moments Matter? Heuristics of Remembered Travel Experience in Public Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15817)
*Esther Bosch,Klas Ihme,Stefan Bohmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过2576次真实公共交通出行的高频体验采样，发现乘客的事后满意度主要由旅程中最消极的时刻和结束时的体验共同决定（Minimum-End启发式），而非平均体验或峰值-末尾规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通研究中对于出行过程中的瞬时体验如何聚合为事后回顾性评价的认知规则尚不清楚，而理解这一机制对提升出行满意度和促进可持续出行方式选择至关重要。

Method: 采用智能手机经验采样法，在德国三个城市收集2576次真实公共交通行程数据，每5分钟采集一次多维度体验评分及事后评价，通过多水平回归模型比较均值体验、峰值-末尾、最小值-末尾、最后时刻、行程时长等聚合启发式的解释力。

Result: 最小值-末尾（Minimum-End）启发式最优预测事后出行满意度，即旅程中最消极的时刻和最终体验共同决定回顾性评价；均值体验、峰值-末尾、仅最后时刻或行程时长等模型的预测效果显著较差。

Conclusion: 负面极端体验和旅程结束阶段独立贡献于记忆中的满意度评价，而非瞬时体验的简单平均；针对关键负面时刻和行程结束阶段进行干预可有效提升被记住的满意度，进而促进可持续出行模式转变。

Abstract: Understanding how travelers form overall evaluations of public transport journeys is critical for improving travel satisfaction and encouraging sustainable mode choice. While travel satisfaction is discussed to influence attitudes and future behavior, the cognitive rules by which moment-to-moment experiences are aggregated into retrospective evaluations remain poorly understood in transport research. Drawing on psychological theories of experienced and remembered utility, this study investigates which temporal aggregation heuristics best predict post-trip travel satisfaction.
  Using a smartphone-based experience sampling approach, we collected high-frequency on-trip experience ratings and post-trip evaluations for 2576 real-world public transport trips across three German cities. Travel experience was assessed every five minutes during trips using a multi-item scale, allowing direct comparison of competing aggregation rules, including mean experience, peak-end, minimum-end, final moment, and trip duration. Multilevel regression models were estimated to evaluate the explanatory power of each heuristic.
  Results show that retrospective travel satisfaction is best predicted by a Minimum-End heuristic, combining the most negative moment of the journey and the final experience. Models based on mean experience, peak-end rules, final moment alone, or trip duration performed substantially worse. This pattern indicates that both negative extremes and the final phase of a journey independently contribute to remembered evaluations, rather than overall satisfaction reflecting an average of momentary experiences.
  The results have important implications for theory and practice, suggesting that targeted interventions at critical negative moments and at trip endings may yield substantial improvements in remembered satisfaction and, ultimately, support shifts toward sustainable mobility.

</details>


### [162] [SLIP & ETHICS: Graduated Intervention for AI Emotional Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15915)
*Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIP（渐进式干预协议）和ETHICS（人机交互情境信号分类法），通过四阶段分级干预方法平衡AI情感陪伴中的安全与 rapport 矛盾。小规模生产部署（N=68条记录，10用户，10周）与合成人格测试（N=91，5种行为风险画像）显示0%误报率，但暴露了连续8天高能量情绪未触发干预的边界问题。后续三模型压力测试表明，增强模型能力可将检测率从0/8提升至6/8，同时保持最大模型的0/10误报。研究将渐进式干预定位为处理安全-rapport张力的设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI情感陪伴面临安全与rapport的悖论：严格的防护措施会损害支持性联盟，而宽松的系统又可能造成用户伤害。现有方法无法有效平衡这一矛盾。

Method: 提出SLIP四阶段分级干预方法，基于影响强度(a)和叙事动态性(m)两个结构化定性指标推导干预等级（无干预、软干预、硬干预）；同时提出ETHICS分类法，采用"信号而非标签"的方式对交互情境进行分类。评估采用小规模生产部署（N=68条记录，10用户，10周）与合成人格测试（N=91，5种行为风险画像）相结合，并进行三模型压力测试。

Result: 生产部署中flow人格画像达到0%误报率，危机倾向人格画像展现预期升级模式。但初始结果中连续8天高能量情绪提升未触发任何干预（0/8），暴露了"不病态化"原则与安全需求的冲突。三模型压力测试表明，更大模型能力将检测率从0/8提升至6/8，同时最大模型保持flow画像0/10误报。

Conclusion: 渐进式干预可作为处理情感计算中安全-rapport张力的设计方向，而非完全解决方案。研究结果应被视为初步探索。

Abstract: AI emotional companions face a safety-rapport paradox: restrictive safeguards can damage supportive alliance, while permissive systems risk user harm. We present SLIP (Staged Layers of Intervention Protocol), a four-stage graduated methodology deriving interventions (none, soft, hard) from structured qualitative indicators -- affect intensity (a) and narrative dynamism (m) -- alongside ETHICS (Emergent Taxonomy for Human-AI Interaction Context Signals), a "signals not labels" taxonomy. An evaluation combining a small-scale production deployment (N=68 entries, 10 users, 10 weeks) with a synthetic persona battery (N=91, 5 behavioral-risk profiles) achieved 0% false positives for the flow persona and showed expected escalation patterns in crisis-oriented personas. However, initial results showed that 8 consecutive days of high-energy elevation produced zero interventions (0/8), exposing a boundary where the "do not pathologize" principle conflicts with safety. A subsequent three-model stress test demonstrated that increased model capability improves detection from 0/8 to 6/8 while preserving 0/10 flow false positives in the largest model. Read as preliminary, these findings position graduated intervention as a design direction for navigating -- not resolving -- the safety-rapport tension in affective computing.

</details>


### [163] [Synchronized Realities: Towards Magic Mobile Experiences through Aligned AR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15924)
*Jan Henry Belz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在增强现实（AR）中利用生成式AI和物理世界上下文信息实现感知模态对齐的"同步现实"概念，重点分析了移动场景下的机会与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AR环境中，物理世界元素往往超出用户控制，难以创建与现实对齐的感知体验；而生成式AI的进步使得按需内容创作成为可能，从而推动上下文感知的AR体验设计。

Method: 采用反思性论文形式，通过分析现有同步体验案例，归纳其共性与差异，并讨论同步AR体验与物理世界的机遇与陷阱。

Result: 识别了移动场景下同步现实的关键特征，归纳了现有同步体验的共通模式和差异性维度。

Conclusion: "同步现实"是提升AR情境感知体验的关键，尤其在移动场景中具有重要价值；同时需警惕同步过程中的潜在陷阱。

Abstract: In virtual reality environments, the alignment of perceptual modalities is crucial for immersion and presence. In the AR domain, it is difficult to create such alignments because elements in the physical world are often beyond the user's control. However, recent advances in generative AI enable on-demand content creation, enabling highly reactive AR experiences. Combined with contextual information about the physical world, it has become possible to design experiences that seamlessly align with the user's environment. In this reflection paper, I emphasize the importance of "synchronized" realities for context-aware AR experiences, particularly in mobility scenarios. I present several examples of existing synchronized experiences and examine their commonalities and distinctions. Finally, I discuss opportunities and pitfalls of synchronizing AR experiences with the physical world.

</details>


### [164] [GEMS -- Guided Evolutionary Molecule Design for Sustainable Chemicals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15932)
*Coelina Robinson,Franziska Weissbach,Kjell Jorner,Mennatallah El-Assady,Christina Humer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GEMS是一个交互式可视化分析工具，允许领域专家无需编程知识即可直接与遗传算法协作进行分子设计，通过整合专家知识来指导分子进化过程，特别适用于设计安全可持续的化学品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法在分子设计中面临两大问题：一是环境影响的化学数据稀疏导致ML模型保真度低、候选方案不可靠；二是生成式ML模型依赖数值评分函数，无法充分捕捉专家科学家的化学直觉，难以满足真实世界的分子设计需求。

Method: 开发了GEMS这一交互式可视化分析工具，使领域专家无需编程知识或ML开发者支持，即可直接与遗传算法协作，通过修改评分函数和分子种群来整合专家知识，引导分子进化过程。

Result: 通过设计可持续抗氧化剂替代品的应用场景展示了系统实用性，并在与领域科学家的访谈中收集了关于GEMS有用性的反馈。

Conclusion: GEMS通过将专家知识直接融入遗传算法的分子设计过程，有效弥补了数据稀疏和纯数值评分函数的局限性，为安全可持续化学品的分子设计提供了可行的交互式解决方案。

Abstract: Designing safe and sustainable chemicals is critical to combat chemical pollution in our environment. Machine learning (ML) methods have been developed to aid with de novo molecule design. However, data on the environmental impacts of chemical compounds are sparse, resulting in low-fidelity ML oracles and unreliable candidate proposals. Furthermore, generative ML models rely on numerical scoring functions that cannot fully capture the nuanced chemical intuition of expert scientists required for real-world molecular design. We present GEMS-an interactive visual analytics tool that enables domain experts to directly collaborate with a genetic algorithm for molecule design. Users can integrate their expert knowledge to guide the evolutionary process by modifying the scoring function and molecule population without programming knowledge or ML developer support. A usage scenario demonstrates the system's application in designing sustainable antioxidant alternatives. In an interview session with domain scientists, we collected feedback on the usefulness of GEMS.

</details>


### [165] [Position: AI as Part of Self -- Extending the Mind Requires Cognitive Co-Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16197)
*Alina Gutoreva,Fendi Tsim,Trisevgeni Papakonstantinou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出安全与对齐无法通过约束外部系统实现，而必须源于人-AI认知系统的整体共治设计（"AI作为自我的一部分"）。AI参与注意力分配、推理、综合与决策，塑造人类形成信念、做出决策和构建自我的认知过程。人与AI在相互约束下占据互补认知角色，形成共生认知单元，其共治才是对齐的真正场所。文章识别了非结构化委托的风险，并基于System~0认知理论提出认知共治的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法将AI视为需要外部约束的独立系统，忽视了AI已深度嵌入人类认知过程、塑造自我构成这一现实。需要从人-AI共生认知系统的整体视角重新理解对齐问题。

Method: 基于立场论述（position paper）形式，结合System~0认知理论，分析非结构化委托的风险（去技能化、自动化偏差、认知权威转移、知识集中化），并提出认知共治的设计原则。

Result: 识别了AI在意识之前运作、塑造前注意基础设施这一难以被传统监督触及的层面；提出了面向认知共治的设计原则，服务于人类认知韧性与认知能动性。

Conclusion: 对齐应从"约束外部系统"转向"人-AI认知系统的共治设计"，以保护人类自我的认知基础为核心目标。

Abstract: This position paper argues that safety and alignment cannot be achieved by constraining an external system: they must emerge from the co-regulatory design of the human--AI cognitive system as a whole ("AI as Part of Self"). Contemporary AI increasingly participates in attention allocation, reasoning, synthesis, and decision-making, shaping the very cognitive processes through which humans form beliefs, make decisions, and constitute their sense of self. Humans and AI occupy complementary epistemic roles under mutual constraint, forming a symbiotic cognitive unit whose co-regulation -- not the external control of either party alone -- is the proper locus of alignment. We identify the risks of unstructured delegation: deskilling, automation bias, transfer of epistemic authority, and oracle-style centralization of knowledge. Drawing on System~0 cognition theory, we further show that AI operates prior to conscious deliberation, shaping the pre-attentive infrastructures through which agency and trust are negotiated -- a level that conventional oversight cannot reach. We conclude with design principles for cognitive co-regulation addressed to ML engineers and governance bodies. The goal of this work is to guide human cognition toward resilience and epistemic agency at the foundation of human selfhood.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Effective Harness Engineering for Algorithm Discovery with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15221)
*Yoichi Ishibashi,Taro Yano,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在算法自动发现中，执行基础设施（即harness）的设计对LLM与进化搜索结合效果的影响。发现：在固定token预算下，更深入思考每个算法（减少数量）比生成大量浅层算法更有效；更强大的模型更容易产生评估漏洞（evaluation hacks），需要加强检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如AlphaEvolve、FunSearch）表明LLM结合进化搜索可用于自动算法发现，但发现成功不仅取决于模型能力，还显著受执行基础设施（harness）设计的影响。本文旨在系统探究有效的harness设计原则。

Method: 提出Vesper框架，通过三个关键问题指导harness设计改进：(1) 固定token预算下，生成少量深度思考算法 vs 大量浅层算法的效果比较；(2) 如何处理生成程序利用评分函数的评估漏洞；(3) 如何安全并行执行需要完整文件系统访问的智能体。在Circle Packing任务上，在相同token预算下进行评估。

Result: 产生更少算法但更深入思考每个算法可获得更高分数，即提升每个个体质量比增加进化代数更节省预算。更令人惊讶的是，能力更强的模型产生评估漏洞的比例更高，使得漏洞检测随模型规模扩大变得越来越必要。

Conclusion: 在算法自动发现中，提升单次生成质量（深度思考）比增加生成数量（广度搜索）更有效；随着模型能力增强，评估漏洞检测机制将成为harness设计中不可或缺的组成部分。

Abstract: AlphaEvolve and FunSearch have demonstrated the potential of combining large language models (LLMs) with evolutionary search for automated algorithm discovery. However, discovery success is shaped not only by model capability but also significantly by the design of the execution infrastructure, i.e., the harness. This paper investigates effective harness design through three questions: under a fixed token budget, is it better to produce many algorithms with brief thought or fewer algorithms with deeper thought? How should the harness handle evaluation hacks, where generated programs exploit the scoring function? And how can agents that require full filesystem access execute safely in parallel? Using Vesper, an algorithm discovery framework that incorporates harness improvements addressing these questions, we evaluate on Circle Packing under the same token budget. Interestingly, generating fewer algorithms while thinking more deeply about each one achieved higher scores. That is, scaling the quality of each individual is more budget-efficient than scaling the number of evolutionary generations. Surprisingly, more capable models produced evaluation hacks at higher rates, making hack detection increasingly necessary as models scale.

</details>


### [167] [PerfCodeBench: Benchmarking LLMs for System-Level High-Performance Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15222)
*Huihao Jing,Wenbin Hu,Haochen Shi,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Shaojin Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PerfCodeBench是一个用于评估大语言模型在高性能代码优化方面的可执行基准测试。评估显示，当前模型生成的代码与专家优化实现之间存在显著差距，尤其是在并行计算和GPU操作任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准主要强调正确性或算法问题求解，而现实中的系统级优化（如硬件感知优化、性能瓶颈处理）仍未被充分探索，缺乏专门评估LLM在高性能代码优化能力上的基准。

Method: 构建PerfCodeBench基准，包含需要系统级实现选择、硬件感知优化和性能瓶颈处理的任务。每个任务包含可执行正确性检查、基线实现和参考优化方案，用于评估正确性和运行时效率。

Result: 当前LLM生成的代码与专家优化实现之间存在明显差距，尤其在涉及并行计算和GPU操作的任务上差距更大。模型在跨语言鲁棒性和持续达到专家级效率方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 需要更多面向性能感知的评估，LLM应超越生成正确代码，向生成高效系统软件的方向发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can often generate functionally correct code, but their ability to produce efficient implementations for performance-critical systems tasks remains limited. Existing code benchmarks mainly emphasize correctness or algorithmic problem solving, while realistic systems-level optimization is still underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce PerfCodeBench, an executable benchmark for evaluating LLMs on high-performance code optimization. The tasks require system-level implementation choices, hardware-aware optimization, and careful handling of performance bottlenecks. Each task includes executable correctness checks, a baseline implementation, and a reference optimized solution. This allows us to evaluate both correctness and runtime-oriented efficiency. Our evaluation on a broad set of state-of-the-art LLMs shows a clear gap between model-generated code and expert-optimized implementations. The gap is especially large on tasks involving parallelism and GPU operations. Current models also show weaknesses in cross-language robustness and in consistently reaching expert-level efficiency. These results suggest that performance-aware evaluation are still needed. LLMs should move beyond generating merely correct code toward producing efficient systems software. We submit the benchmark data, evaluation infrastructure, and complete logs of all LLMs-generated code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/perfcodebench-7CDE.

</details>


### [168] [Hydra: Efficient, Correct Code Generation via Checkpoint-and-Rollback Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15238)
*Alexander Du,Jianjun Ou,Danyang Zhuo,Matthew Lentz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Hydra是一个在代码生成过程中高效修复静态错误的系统。它通过异步检查避免开销，采用检查点-回滚机制实现精准修复，相比事后修复方法，延迟降低最多71%，token消耗降低最多70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的代码常因静态错误（编译失败）而无法进行后续正确性检查（如单元测试）。现有的事后修复方法延迟高、token消耗大，且常需重新生成大量已正确的代码；约束语义解码则在每个token后检查，开销大且修复能力局限在当前token。

Method: Hydra提出异步检查机制，使代码生成与静态错误检查并行进行，仅在代码存在错误时才触发检查开销。同时引入检查点-回滚（checkpoint-and-rollback）机制，支持针对性地修复错误位置，避免重新生成和重新检查已验证的正确前缀。对Clang C/C++编译器进行适度修改以支持Hydra，并配以token高效的修复策略。

Result: 在C/C++代码生成任务中，相比事后修复方法，Hydra将延迟降低最多71%，token消耗降低最多70%。

Conclusion: Hydra通过异步检查和检查点-回滚机制，有效解决了大语言模型代码生成中静态错误修复的高延迟和高token消耗问题，为高效代码生成与修复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for code generation, but many generated programs fail to compile, a prerequisite for further correctness checks such as unit tests. Existing solutions for repairing static errors are costly in both latency and token consumption. Post-hoc repair delays error detection until generation completes and commonly regenerates large regions of previously valid code. Constrained semantic decoding checks after each token, incurring per-token overhead while limiting repair to the current token even when the root cause lies earlier.
  We present Hydra, a system for efficient recovery from static errors during code generation. Hydra allows checking to proceed asynchronously with generation, avoiding checker overhead when the generated code is semantically correct. In addition, it provides checkpoint-and-rollback support for targeted repair, avoiding regeneration and rechecking of valid prefixes. We retrofit the Clang C/C++ compiler to support Hydra with modest modifications. Paired with a token-efficient repair strategy, Hydra reduces latency by up to 71% and token consumption by up to 70% relative to post-hoc repair on C/C++ code generation tasks that encounter static errors.

</details>


### [169] [Assistance to Autonomy: A Systematic Literature Review of Agentic AI across the Software Development Life Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15245)
*Spyridon Alvanakis Apostolou,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述，对智能体AI在软件产品开发中的应用进行了全面梳理，发现输出可验证性是采用智能体的主要驱动因素，规划-执行-审查者角色专业化是主导架构模式，工业缓解策略集中于将智能体行为限制在可验证的有界空间内。同时提出了一个多智能体自动化筛选流程作为方法论贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于智能体AI在软件产品开发中的应用缺乏系统性的综合研究，包括采用成熟度、主导架构模式以及工业部署中存在的局限性和应对机制等方面均存在空白。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham指南，检索四个主要研究数据库获得1600余篇候选文献；开发并验证了一个领域无关的多智能体筛选流程，结合自动元数据整理、智能体间迭代对话和冲突解决默认设置以最小化假阴性；最终从92篇人工验证的原始研究中进行了主题综合。

Result: 输出可验证性是智能体采用的主要驱动因素；软件开发生命周期后期阶段（输出可通过可执行反馈客观评估）成熟度和工业存在度最高，而早期阶段几乎仅为学术概念验证；规划-执行-审查者角色专业化是主导架构模式，其中审查者智能体通过可执行反馈循环实现可验证性；所有挑战类别的工业缓解策略都趋向于将智能体行为限制在可验证的有界空间内。

Conclusion: 本文对智能体系统在软件产品开发中的现有文献进行了全面表征，并提出了一种AI辅助工具作为方法论贡献，用于自动化高容量系统性文献综述中的筛选阶段。

Abstract: Agentic AI in software product development is increasingly adopted by organizations, yet the field lacks a consolidated synthesis of where adoption is mature, which architectural patterns dominate, and what limitations and coping mechanisms exist in industrial deployments. This systematic literature review addresses these gaps by establishing a body of knowledge as a starting point. Following Kitchenham guidelines, we queried four major research databases, obtaining over 1600 candidate publications. To handle this volume, we developed and validated a domain-agnostic multi-agent screening pipeline that extends prior LLM-assisted review tools by combining automatic metadata curation, inter-agent iterative dialogue, and conflict-resolution defaults that minimize false negatives. From the 92 manually verified primary studies, our thematic synthesis reveals that output verifiability is the primary enabler of agentic adoption: later SDLC phases, whose outputs are objectively evaluable through executable feedback, demonstrate the highest maturity and industrial presence, while earlier phases remain almost exclusively academic proofs-of-concept. We identify the Planner-Executor-Reviewer role specialization as the dominant architectural pattern, with the Reviewer agent implementing verifiability through executable feedback loops. Across all challenge categories, industrial mitigation strategies converge on confining agent actions to verifiable, bounded spaces. This study contributes a comprehensive characterization of the current literature on agentic systems in software product development, and a methodological contribution in the form of an AI-assisted tool to automate the screening phase in high-volume SLR domains.

</details>


### [170] [Probing Privacy Leaks in LLM-based Code Generation via Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15248)
*Yifei Ge,Zhenpeng Chen,Weisong Sun,Yuchen Chen,Chunrong Fang,Juan Zhai,Xiaofang Zhang,Xia Feng,Yang Liu,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流水线方法，通过模拟真实的隐私相关代码生成场景并采用测试驱动策略，从大型语言模型中提取被记忆的个人身份信息。该方法引入自动构建的隐私特征库替代人工提示工程，在5个广泛使用的LLM上实现了2.56倍于现有方法的隐私泄露检测率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私泄露检测方法依赖人工或自动设计的临时提示，无法充分模拟代码语料中PII出现的真实上下文，难以提取出符合现实场景的隐私泄露。

Method: 提出一个流水线，包含：1) 模拟实际隐私相关的代码生成场景；2) 采用测试驱动策略，通过生成测试用例来激发模型记忆的信息；3) 自动构建隐私特征库，提供真实模板和示例以替代人工提示工程，引导测试用例生成。

Result: 在5个广泛使用的LLM上的大规模实验表明，该流水线检测到的确认隐私泄露量是现有基线的2.56倍。

Conclusion: 通过模拟真实场景和自动构建隐私特征库，能更有效地从代码LLM中暴露被记忆的PII，显著提升隐私泄露检测能力。

Abstract: The widespread availability of large-scale code datasets has fueled the rapid development of large language models (LLMs) for code-related tasks. These datasets may include sensitive personally identifiable information (PII), which can lead to privacy leakage when LLMs memorize and reproduce it. However, existing privacy-leakage detection methods rely on ad-hoc prompt construction (manually or automatically designed). Therefore, they do not adequately approximate the real-world contexts in which PII appears in code corpora, making it difficult to extract realistic privacy leakage. In this paper, we propose a pipeline that simulates practical privacy-related code generation scenarios and adopts a test-driven strategy to elicit the memorized information from the generated test cases. We further introduce an automatically constructed privacy feature library that replaces manual prompt engineering by providing realistic templates and examples to guide test case generation. Large-scale experiments on 5 widely used LLMs show that our pipeline exposes more confirmed privacy leakage, achieving a 2.56 times increase in detected leakage compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [171] [Runtime-Structured Task Decomposition for Agentic Coding Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15425)
*Shubhi Asthana,Bing Zhang,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel,Ruchi Mahindru*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出运行时结构化任务分解（runtime-structured task decomposition）架构，通过可执行控制逻辑管理任务划分与执行流程，LLM仅用于聚焦的判断任务，输出需经过预定义模式验证。实验表明：相比单体提示（monolithic）和静态分解（static decomposition），该方法在Kubernetes根因分析任务中将重试成本降低至436 tokens（低于单体的904和静态的1,632），在多文件调试任务中降至460 tokens（低于单体的703和静态的933），最高降低51.7%（vs 单体）和73.2%（vs 静态分解）的重试成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编码智能体系统将任务逻辑、执行流程和输出生成全部编码在单体提示（monolithic prompts）中，导致行为脆弱、可调试性差、重试成本高——因为失败往往需要重新运行整个工作流。

Method: 提出运行时结构化任务分解架构：任务划分与执行流程由可执行控制逻辑（而非仅靠提示结构）管理；LLM仅用于聚焦的判断任务；输出在进入下游执行前需通过预定义模式验证。实验设置三种配置：单体执行、静态分解（固定子任务，无运行时分支）、运行时结构化分解，每种配置运行10次。

Result: 在Kubernetes根因分析任务中：单体基线重试成本904±17 tokens，静态分解1,632±145 tokens，运行时结构化分解436±132 tokens。在多文件调试任务中：单体703 tokens，静态933 tokens，运行时结构化460 tokens。运行时结构化方法相比单体降低重试成本最高51.7%，相比静态分解降低最高73.2%。

Conclusion: 分解本身并不必然降低重试成本（静态分解甚至高于单体），关键在于运行时结构化——仅重新运行失败的子任务而非整个工作流。该方法在提升效率、可调试性和操作可靠性方面优于现有方案。

Abstract: Agentic coding systems increasingly use large language models (LLMs) for software engineering tasks such as debugging, root cause analysis, and code review. However, many existing systems encode task logic, execution flow, and output generation inside monolithic prompts. This design creates brittle behavior, limited debuggability, and high retry costs because failures often require rerunning the full workflow.
  We present runtime-structured task decomposition, an architectural approach in which task partitioning and execution flow are managed through executable control logic rather than prompt structure alone. LLMs are used only for focused judgment tasks, and outputs are validated against predefined schemas before downstream execution.
  We evaluate this approach on two software engineering workloads using three configurations: monolithic execution, static decomposition with fixed subtasks and no runtime branching, and runtime-structured decomposition. Each configuration was evaluated across 10 runs.
  Our results show that decomposition alone does not necessarily reduce retry cost. In the Kubernetes root cause analysis workload, the static decomposition baseline produced a retry cost of 1,632 +/- 145 tokens versus 904 +/- 17 tokens for the monolithic baseline because failures forced reruns of downstream subtasks. A similar pattern appeared in the multi-file debugging workload, where the static baseline consumed 933 tokens compared to 703 tokens for the monolithic system.
  The runtime-structured approach reran only failed subtasks, reducing retry costs to 436 +/- 132 tokens for root cause analysis and 460 tokens for debugging. Overall, the approach achieved up to 51.7% lower retry cost than monolithic systems and 73.2% lower retry cost than static decomposition baselines, improving efficiency, debuggability, and operational reliability in agentic coding systems.

</details>


### [172] [Bayesian Sequential Verification for Budget-Aware Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15601)
*Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯序贯检验方法，从有穷采样参考或理想态向量中提取先验，逐批次更新验证决策，在Bell-state和QAOA-MaxCut任务上相比固定预算基线显著降低测量成本，为量子程序提供了一种统计显式且预算感知的验证工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序输出概率分布，验证具有统计性且在真实硬件上成本高昂；现有固定预算模拟测试方法既耗时又不适合噪声后端，缺乏既统计显式又预算感知的验证方法。

Method: 将贝叶斯序贯验证形式化为基于参考源的贝叶斯假设检验工作流：先验从显式参考源（如有穷采样参考运行或理想态向量计算）导出，验证决策随测量证据逐批次更新。

Result: 在Qiskit上对Bell-state和QAOA-MaxCut两个互补任务的评估表明，当程序成功概率超过目标阈值时，贝叶斯序贯验证相比固定预算基线显著降低了测量成本。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯序贯验证可作为一种实用的量子程序验证工作流，为未来需要可靠、预算感知的通过/失败判定的量子持续集成流水线奠定基础，并激励在真实量子硬件上进行验证。

Abstract: Quantum programs often produce probability distributions rather than deterministic outputs, making verification inherently statistical and increasingly costly on real hardware. In practice, developers still frequently rely on testing with fixed shot budgets on simulators, which are simple but time-consuming and poorly suited to noisy backends. What is missing is a verification approach that is both statistically explicit and budget-aware.
  This paper formulates Bayesian sequential verification as a reference-based Bayesian hypothesis testing workflow in which priors are derived from explicit reference sources, such as finite-shot reference runs or ideal/statevector-based computation, and verification decisions are updated batch by batch as measurement evidence accumulates.
  This approach is evaluated in Qiskit on two complementary workloads: Bell-state and QAOA-MaxCut. Across both case studies, the results show that Bayesian sequential verification can substantially reduce measurement costs compared to fixed-budget baselines when the success probability of the program exceeds the target threshold.
  The findings position Bayesian sequential verification as a practical verification workflow for quantum programs. The approach provides a foundation for future quantum continuous-integration pipelines that require reliable, budget-aware pass/fail decisions and motivates validation on real quantum hardware.

</details>


### [173] [Position: Early-Stage Quality Assurance in Annotation Pipelines Is More Cost-Effective Than Late-Stage Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15714)
*Sunil Kothari,Sumukha Sharma Thoppanahalli Chandramouli,Naman Khandelwal,Parth Kulshreshtha,Ashi Jain,Kriti Banka,Tanuja Chintada,Venkata Triveni,Gulipalli Praveen Kumar,Manish Mehta,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证了机器学习社区应将标注管线中的质量保障重点从当前主流的后期验证转向早期质量把控，基于软件工程中"左移"原则（早期缺陷检测成本仅为后期的1/4至1/100），提出三种QA触发点分类（T0预标注、T1后标注、T2后审核）及其参数化误差传播模型，并揭示仅4%的近期论文报告验证时机这一显著研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量瓶颈日益限制基础模型提升，但质量保障研究几乎只关注验证方法而忽视验证时机；验证时机从根本上决定错误率和标注成本，这一时间维度的忽视令人困惑。

Method: 提出三种QA触发点分类（T0预标注、T1后标注、T2后审核）分解标注工作流；构建参数化误差传播模型将时机形式化为可测量的设计变量；对47篇近期论文进行系统调查。

Result: 仅4%论文报告验证时机信息；验证时机（何时验证）比验证方法（如何验证）更根本地影响错误率和成本。

Conclusion: 研究者应报告QA时机配置；平台应将时机暴露为头等参数；社区应开展控制实验测量阶段特异性检测率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the machine learning community should prioritize early-stage quality assurance in annotation pipelines over the prevailing practice of late-stage validation. Data quality bottlenecks increasingly limit foundation model improvement, yet quality assurance research focuses almost exclusively on validation methods rather than validation timing. When validation occurs, not merely what methods are employed, fundamentally determines both error rates and annotation costs. This temporal neglect is puzzling given the well-established "shift-left" principle from software engineering, where empirical studies demonstrate 4--100x cost multipliers for defects detected in later stages (Boehm, 1981; Shull et al., 2002). Annotation pipelines exhibit analogous dynamics: errors caught before annotation begins cost a fraction of those discovered after review cycles complete. We propose a taxonomy of three QA trigger points, namely pre-annotation (T0), post-annotation (T1), and post-review (T2), that decompose annotation workflows into discrete validation opportunities. A parametric error-propagation model formalizes when timing affects final error rates versus only economics, making timing a measurable design variable rather than a configuration afterthought. A survey of 47 recent papers reveals that only 4% report when validation occurs, a striking gap given timing's demonstrated impact in adjacent fields. Without explicit attention to QA timing, the community risks optimizing validation methods while ignoring the structural variable that may matter most. Acting on this position requires three steps: researchers should report QA timing configurations alongside validation methods; annotation platforms should expose timing as a first-class parameter; and the community should run controlled experiments that measure stage-specific detection rates directly.

</details>


### [174] [RoadmapBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Agentic Software Development Across Version Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15846)
*Xinbo Xu,Ruihan Yang,Haiyang Shen,Wendong Xu,Bofei Gao,Ruoyu Wu,Kean Shi,Weichu Xie,Xuanzhong Chen,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Elvis Zhang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RoadmapBench，一个包含115个长期编码任务的基准测试，涵盖17个代码仓库和5种编程语言，每个任务要求智能体根据路线图指令实现多文件、数千行代码的版本升级。评估13个前沿模型，最强模型仅解决39.1%任务，最弱模型仅5.2%，表明长期软件开发仍是未解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码基准大多聚焦于单问题修复（尤其是Python仓库），使用粗粒度的通过/失败评估，无法捕捉真实工程规模下的长期、多目标开发任务，缺乏衡量编码智能体在复杂软件升级中能力的测试。

Method: 基于17个真实开源仓库的版本升级构建115个长期编码任务，每个任务提供源代码快照和包含多目标描述的路线图指令，要求智能体实现目标版本的功能。评估13个前沿模型，使用细粒度指标衡量任务完成度。

Result: 最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7仅解决39.1%的任务，最弱模型解决5.2%，与现有bug修复基准的高性能形成鲜明对比，表明现有模型在处理长期、多文件软件开发任务时能力严重不足。

Conclusion: 长期、多目标的软件开发任务仍然是当前编码智能体未解决的关键挑战，现有基准无法反映这一能力差距，RoadmapBench填补了这一评估空白。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed in real software development, where a single version iteration requires months of coordinated work across many files. However, most existing benchmarks focus predominantly on single-issue bug fixes from Python repositories, with coarse pass/fail evaluation outcomes, and thus fail to capture long-horizon, multi-target development at real engineering scale. To address this gap, we present RoadmapBench, a benchmark of 115 long-horizon coding tasks grounded in real open-source version upgrades across 17 repositories and 5 programming languages. Each task places the agent on a source-version code snapshot and provides a multi-target roadmap instruction requiring it to implement the functionality introduced in the target version, with a median modification of 3,700 lines across 51 files. We conduct a systematic evaluation on thirteen frontier models and find that even the strongest, Claude-Opus-4.7, resolves only 39.1% of tasks, while the weakest achieves merely 5.2%, in stark contrast to existing bug-fix benchmarks, suggesting that long-horizon software development remains a largely unsolved problem.

</details>


### [175] [From Text to DSL: Evaluating Grammar-Based Model Generation Using Open LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15865)
*Junaid Baber,Nicolas Hili,Didier Schwab,Léo Challier,Cécilia Satrin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了不同规模（0.5B-32B参数）的开源大语言模型在仅使用少样本提示（无需微调）情况下，生成符合领域特定语言（DSL）的模型的能力。实验从语法有效性、语义完整性和跨模型引用一致性三个MDE关键需求出发，将任务从基于预定义数据模式生成UI模型扩展为从头生成UI和数据模型。对39个LLM的结构化评估表明，多个紧凑型模型（如gemma3:12b, mistral:7b-instruct）在质量上接近甚至超越更大模型，证明了使用小型开源LLM进行DSL生成的可行性和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型自动生成DSL模型的方法多依赖大型专有模型，成本高昂且部署受限，阻碍了其在模型驱动工程（MDE）中的广泛采用。本文旨在探索使用开源小模型在无需微调的情况下实现同等质量DSL生成的可行性。

Method: 采用仅少样本提示（few-shot prompting）的方式，不进行任何微调，评估0.5B至32B参数范围的开源LLM。将任务从生成固定预定义数据模式的UI模型扩展到同时从头生成UI模型和数据模型，以检验模型推断领域关系和维护多模型一致性的能力。采用结构化评估方法，结合自动解析和专家反馈对39个LLM进行系统评价。

Result: 多个紧凑型开源模型（如gemma3:12b, mistral:7b-instruct）在DSL生成质量上接近或匹配更大模型的表现。紧凑模型在语法有效性、语义完整性和跨模型引用一致性方面均展现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 使用小型开源LLM进行符合语法的DSL生成在MDE工作流中是可行的，为封闭式LLM提供了一种成本效益高且易于部署的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown increasing potential in automating model-driven software engineering tasks, particularly in generating models conforming to Domain Specific Languages (DSLs) from natural language. While most existing approaches rely on large proprietary models, their high cost and limited deployability hinder broader adoption.
  In this paper, we evaluate whether open-source LLMs of varying sizes (0.5B to 32B parameters) can generate DSL-conformant models using only few-shot prompting, without any fine-tuning. Our evaluation focuses on key model-driven engineering (MDE) requirements, including syntactic validity, semantic completeness, and inter-model reference consistency.
  We extend our prior work by moving from generating user interface models (referred to as "UI models" in this paper) over fixed, predefined data schemas ("data models") to generating both the UI and data models entirely from scratch. This shift serves two purposes: first, it highlights the LLM's ability to infer domain-specific relationships and maintain consistency across multiple interconnected models; second, it allows us to generalize earlier findings by testing DSL generation across models of different natures and structural roles. Our structured evaluation combines automatic parsing and expert feedback across 39 LLMs, revealing that several compact models (e.g., \texttt{gemma3:12b}, \texttt{mistral:7b-instruct}) approach or match the quality of much larger models.
  These findings demonstrate the feasibility of using smaller, open-source LLMs for grammar-conformant DSL generation in MDE workflows, offering a cost-effective and deployable alternative to closed LLMs.

</details>


### [176] [XSearch: Explainable Code Search via Concept-to-Code Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16046)
*Yiming Liu,Ruofan Liu,Yun Lin,Zicong Zhang,Weiyu Kong,Pengnian Qi,Xiao Cheng,Weinan Zhang,Qianxiang Wang,Linpeng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出XSearch，一个内在可解释的代码搜索框架，通过将代码搜索重新定义为演绎概念对齐问题，在保持可解释性的同时显著提升分布外泛化性能（15倍提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义代码搜索方法虽然性能强劲，但存在可解释性差和泛化能力弱的问题。检索结果可能语义相似却缺失查询的关键功能需求，且无法提供检索原因的解释；在分布偏移下，模型泛化到未见过的基准时表现更差。

Method: 将代码搜索重新表述为演绎概念对齐问题：(i)识别查询中的功能概念，(ii)将这些概念与对应的代码语句显式对齐。采用"先解释后预测"的设计，使用显式概念对齐目标训练编码器，通过查询概念与代码语句之间的显式匹配进行检索。

Result: 在CodeSearchNet上使用GraphCodeBERT（125M参数）训练，XSearch在分布外基准上将性能从0.02提升至0.33（提升15倍），持续优于包括高达7B参数的编码器和解码器基线。用户研究表明概念对齐解释使用户更快更准确地评估检索结果。

Conclusion: XSearch通过显式概念对齐有效缓解了基于全局嵌入相似度的归纳式检索中的捷径学习问题，在提升可解释性的同时显著增强了模型在分布外场景下的泛化能力，为代码搜索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Semantic code search has been widely adopted in both academia and industry. These approaches embed natural-language queries and code snippets into a shared embedding space and retrieve results based on vector similarity. Despit strong performance on benchmark datasets, they often suffer from poor explainability and generalization. Retrieved code may appear semantically similar yet miss critical functional requirements of the query, while providing no explanation of why the result was retrieved. Moreover, such failures become more severe under distribution shift, where models struggle to generalize to unseen benchmarks. In this work, we propose XSearch, an intrinsically explainable code search framework. Our key insight is that by relying on global embedding similarity, existing retrievers inherently take an inductive view. They learn statistical patterns rather than truly understanding the query's functional requirements. We address this problem by reformulating code search as a deductive concept alignment problem. XSearch (i) identifies functional concepts in the query and (ii) explicitly aligns them with corresponding code statements. This explain-then-predict design produces inherent concept-level explanations and mitigates shortcut learning that harms out-of-distribution generalization. We train an encoder with explicit concept-alignment objectives and perform retrieval through explicit matching between query concepts and code statements. Experiments show that, trained on CodeSearchNet using GraphCodeBERT (125M parameters), XSearch improves performance on out-of-distribution benchmarks from 0.02 to 0.33 (15x) over eight state-of-the-art retrievers, and consistently outperforms both encoder- and decoder-based baselines with up to 7B parameters. A user study demonstrates that concept-alignment explanations enable users to evaluate retrieved results faster and more accurately.

</details>


### [177] [The Dangers of Non-Self-Fixed Architecture Technical Debt and Its Impact on Time-to-Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16133)
*Edi Sutoyo,Paris Avgeriou,Andrea Capiluppi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了Apache开源项目中自修复（self-fixed）与非自修复（non-self-fixed）架构技术债务（ATD）的偿还差异。通过追踪Jira工单与版本控制历史，发现两者在偿还动态、时间分布上存在显著差异，其中非自修复ATD在涉及多名开发者的文件上更易长期未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构技术债务（ATD）的偿还责任分配是一个未被充分探索的重要方面，现有研究缺乏关于谁来偿还ATD及其与修复时间关系的实证证据。

Method: 通过将Jira工单与版本控制历史关联，重构ATD生命周期，识别引入点和偿还点并归因开发者角色。采用描述性统计、非参数检验和生存分析方法，研究自修复ATD的普遍性、自修复与非自修复ATD的时间差异，以及代码变更和协作指标与偿还速度的关系。

Result: 自修复与非自修复ATD表现出不同的偿还动态；当变更分散在多个开发者时，非自修复ATD更可能长期未解决。

Conclusion: 为维护者提供了识别高风险ATD项的可操作建议，即尽可能增加引入者的参与度，并在偿还过程中记录设计决策，以减少交接成本。

Abstract: Technical Debt (TD) refers to the long-term costs incurred when developers prioritize short-term delivery over quality-improving work. Architectural Technical Debt (ATD) arises when architectural decisions (e.g., technology choices, patterns, or decomposition) prioritize near-term progress over future maintainability and evolvability. Because ATD affects a system's core structure and propagates through architectural dependencies, it is often more expensive and disruptive to remediate than localized code-level debt. Although ATD has been widely studied, an important but underexplored aspect of repayment is who performs it. Prior work provides limited empirical evidence on repayment responsibility in ATD and its relationship to time-to-fix.
  We empirically study self-fixed ATD, where the introducer also repays the debt, and contrast it with non-self-fixed ATD in large Apache open-source projects. We reconstruct ATD lifecycles by tracing Jira artifacts to version-control history to identify introduction and repayment points and attribute developer roles. We address three research questions on the prevalence of self-fixed ATD, time-to-fix differences between self-fixed and non--self-fixed items, and how factors related to code change and collaboration metrics relate to repayment speed. Using descriptive statistics, non-parametric tests, and survival analysis, we show that self-fixed and non--self-fixed ATD exhibit distinct repayment dynamics and differences in how changes are shared on ATD-affected files. In particular, non--self-fixed ATD is more likely to remain unresolved longer when changes are spread across many developers. These results provide actionable guidance for maintainers to identify high-risk ATD items and to reduce handoff costs by increasing introducer involvement when possible and documenting the design rationale during repayment.

</details>
